########## ./README.md ##########
# Lorenz Flow ⚡

Lorenz Flow is a comprehensive, premium productivity and security companion for Android. Designed to streamline your daily workflow, the app combines task management, color-coded notes, a calendar planner, a Pomodoro focus timer, and a hardware-encrypted password vault into a single, cohesive experience.

Built with **Java**, **Room Database (SQLite)**, and **Material Design 3**, Lorenz Flow is designed for offline-first reliability, high performance, and robust security.

---

## 📱 Features Breakdown

### 1. Task & Deadline Manager
*   **Dynamic Task Management:** Create, edit, delete, and share tasks.
*   **Archiving System:** Move tasks to a dedicated Archive section to declutter your list without losing your history, with restore and clear-all operations.
*   **Categorization:** Label tasks using pre-defined categories with visually distinct badges:
    *   *Work, Personal, School, Wellness, Urgent, General*.
*   **Sorting Options:** Sort active tasks by *Upcoming Deadline* (default), *Newest First*, or *Oldest First*.
*   **Swipe Gestures:** Swipe-to-delete gesture using `ItemTouchHelper` with an instant snackbar notification containing a functional **Undo** mechanism.
*   **Completed Tasks Drawer:** Collapsible completed section showcasing task counts and a one-tap option to permanently purge all completed tasks.
*   **PDF Report Exporting:** Generates an A4 PDF document containing all active tasks and deadlines, styled and structured dynamically via graphics drawing canvas and exported directly to your device's `Documents` folder.

### 2. Rich Text Notes
*   **Grid Presentation:** Rendered in a responsive 2-column staggered grid layout.
*   **Color Customization:** Dynamic theme-aware palette selection to assign custom colors to notes, which adapt smoothly to dark and light modes.
*   **Manual Reordering:** Drag-and-drop notes in any direction to custom-arrange them. Layout positions are saved instantly in the background.
*   **Long-Press Menu:** Quick actions to Archive or Delete note cards directly.

### 3. Calendar Planner
*   **Visual Planner:** Integrated datepicker calendar widget showing due tasks.
*   **Due Date Filtering:** Selecting any date instantly queries the database to show all tasks scheduled for completion on that exact day.
*   **Direct Modifications:** Edit, complete, delete, or share tasks straight from the calendar list.

### 4. Pomodoro Focus Timer
*   **Multiple Modes:** Supports *Focus Session* (25 min default), *Short Break* (5 min default), and *Long Break* (15 min default).
*   **Custom Durations:** Adjust session lengths to fit your personal workflow.
*   **Visual Indicators:** Circular progress indicators tracking elapsed time percentage, colored dynamically according to the active mode.
*   **Alert & Vibration Alarms:** Triggers continuous ringing and system vibration when a session ends, complemented by high-priority push notifications.
*   **Smart Auto-Dismiss:** Overlay control dialog allowing users to shut off alarms, with a 15-second automatic termination fail-safe to prevent battery drain.

### 5. Hardware-Encrypted Password Manager
*   **Credential Vault:** Store and manage login credentials (accounts, usernames, and passwords) securely in a local database.
*   **Search Filter:** Instantly filter your saved credentials by website title or username.
*   **Secure Password Generator:** Generate strong, random passwords (16-characters, mixing uppercase, lowercase, numbers, and special symbols) using cryptographically secure algorithms.
*   **Biometric/Device Lock Integration:** Authenticates access using Android's system lock (Fingerprint, Face, PIN, or Pattern) via `KeyguardManager`.
*   **Custom Master PIN Fallback:** If the device has no secure lock set up, the app falls back to a custom 4-digit Master PIN, hashed securely with SHA-256.
*   **State-of-the-Art Encryption:** Passwords are never stored in plain text. They are encrypted using **AES/CBC/PKCS7Padding** with a hardware-backed key generated in Android's KeyStore Provider. Unique, randomized Initialization Vectors (IV) are generated and stored alongside each entry to prevent cryptographic pattern attacks.

### 6. Profile & Customization
*   **Custom Persona:** Personalize the app with your name, bio, and custom avatar.
*   **Persistent Offline Media:** Avatars are imported using modern Activity Contracts and copied locally into the app's private directory (`filesDir`) as `profile_pic.jpg`, resolving URI permission-loss issues and ensuring avatars render offline.
*   **Theme Control:** Supports Light mode, Dark mode, and automatic System-following behavior.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack & Architecture

```mermaid
graph TD
    UI[MainActivity - Single Activity Wrapper]
    
    %% UI Components
    UI --> TF[TasksFragment]
    UI --> NF[NotesFragment]
    UI --> CF[CalendarFragment]
    UI --> PF[PomodoroFragment]
    UI --> SF[SettingsFragment]
    
    %% Sub-fragments
    SF --> ProfileF[ProfileFragment]
    SF --> PMF[PasswordManagerFragment]
    SF --> AboutF[AboutFragment]
    TF --> ATF[ArchiveTasksFragment]
    NF --> ANF[ArchiveNotesFragment]
    
    %% Utilities & Managers
    TF --> PDF[PdfExportUtil]
    PMF --> SEC[SecurityUtil]
    SEC --> AKS[Android KeyStore AES/CBC/PKCS7]
    
    %% Alarms & Notifications
    TF --> AM[AlarmManager]
    AM --> AR[AlarmReceiver]
    BR[BootReceiver] -->|On Reboot| AM
    AR --> NH[NotificationHelper]
    PF --> NH
    
    %% Database layer
    TF & NF & CF & PMF & ATF & ANF & BR --> DB[(Room Database lorenz_flow_db)]
    DB --> TaskDao[TaskDao]
    DB --> NoteDao[NoteDao]
    DB --> PasswordDao[PasswordDao]
```

### 1. Architecture Pattern
*   **Single-Activity Architecture:** The application uses a single main activity container (`MainActivity`) with sub-fragments switched in and out of the view hierarchy, keeping navigation transitions smooth and memory footprint low.
*   **View Binding:** Direct layout element references via generated binding classes, eliminating `findViewById` boilerplate and preventing null pointer errors during fragment transitions.
*   **Multithreading:** Database transactions and heavy tasks (such as PDF generation, photo importing, or password calculations) are run off the main UI thread using `Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor()` to prevent UI stuttering or ANR (App Not Responding) dialogs.

### 2. Database & Data Storage (Room ORM)
*   **Room Database Framework:** Abstracts raw SQLite operations into clean compile-time verified queries.
*   **Schema Schema Version:** Current Database Version is **4**.
*   **Safe Migrations:** Implements structured `Migration` scripts to safely update schemas between releases. The transition from **v3 to v4** adds the `archived` column (integers representing boolean states) to `tasks` and `notes` tables dynamically without erasing existing user data.

### 3. Alarm Scheduling & System Receivers
*   **Exact Timers:** Schedules task reminders using Android's `AlarmManager` (`setExactAndAllowWhileIdle`) targeted at the precise epoch millisecond of the deadline.
*   **Push Notifications:** Alerts are built via `NotificationCompat.Builder` using high-priority notification channels, ensuring reminders break through system battery optimizations.
*   **Boot Survivability:** Registers a `BootReceiver` listening for `android.intent.action.BOOT_COMPLETED`. Upon device reboot, it retrieves all incomplete tasks with future deadlines in a background thread and automatically registers new exact alarms in the system OS.

---

## 🔒 Security Implementation (Password Manager)

Lorenz Flow features an offline credential vault that operates under zero-trust principles:

1.  **Hardware-Backed Key Generation:**
    On first launch of the password manager, the app requests the system's `AndroidKeyStore` provider to generate a secret key using the standard Advanced Encryption Standard (AES) protocol. The key generation parameter specification is locked to blocks of CBC and PKCS7 padding.
2.  **Symmetric Encryption:**
    When saving a credential, the plaintext password is encrypted using the key stored in the hardware module. The algorithm returns the ciphertext and the randomly generated Initialization Vector (IV).
3.  **Encrypted Insertion:**
    The Room database only receives and stores the account title, username, ciphertext (base64 encoded), and the IV (base64 encoded). The plaintext password is never cached in local memory or written to disk.
4.  **Hardware Decryption:**
    To display a password, the system requests authorization, retrieves the ciphertext and IV from SQLite, loads the key from the hardware-backed keystore, initializes the Cipher object in Decrypt Mode with the IV, and decrypts the string on demand.

## 📋 Requirements & Dependencies

To compile and run this project, your environment must satisfy the following criteria:

### System & Build Requirements
*   **Java Development Kit (JDK):** Version 17
*   **Gradle Build System:** Gradle Version `9.4.1`
*   **Android Gradle Plugin (AGP):** Version `9.2.1`
*   **Android SDK Platforms:** Compile SDK version `34`, Target SDK version `34`
*   **Minimum Target Support:** Min SDK version `26` (Android 8.0 Oreo and above)

### Key Core Dependencies (`app/build.gradle`)
*   `androidx.appcompat:appcompat:1.6.1` — Backward-compatible action bar and theme support.
*   `com.google.android.material:material:1.11.0` — Material Design 3 interactive UI widgets.
*   `androidx.room:room-runtime:2.6.1` — Room local object-relational mapping database.
*   `androidx.room:room-compiler:2.6.1` — Room annotation processor for database compilation.
*   `androidx.recyclerview:recyclerview:1.3.2` — Memory-efficient list displays.
*   `androidx.constraintlayout:constraintlayout:2.1.4` — Flat, responsive UI layouts.

---

## 🚀 Setup & Execution Guide

Follow these steps to set up, build, and run the Lorenz Flow project on your machine:

### 1. Import Project
1.  Download or clone the project repository.
2.  Open **Android Studio** (Koala or newer recommended).
3.  Select **File > Open** (or **Open an Existing Project** on the welcome screen).
4.  Navigate to the root directory `lorenzFlow` and click **OK**.

### 2. Configure SDK Locations
If Android Studio doesn't automatically locate your Android SDK, create a file named `local.properties` in the root folder of the project (if it doesn't already exist) and specify your SDK path:
```properties
sdk.dir=C\:\\Users\\YourUsername\\AppData\\Local\\Android\\Sdk
```
*(Replace `YourUsername` with your actual Windows account user folder name).*

### 3. Sync Gradle
1.  Wait for Android Studio to initialize.
2.  Click the **Sync Project with Gradle Files** button (represented by an elephant with a circular arrow icon in the toolbar, or select **File > Sync Project with Gradle Files**).
3.  Ensure your internet connection is active so Gradle can fetch the Room ORM and Material library components.

### 4. Build and Run
#### Via Android Studio (Recommended):
1.  Connect your physical Android device via USB (with **Developer Options** and **USB Debugging** enabled) or launch an Android Virtual Device (AVD) Emulator (Targeting API level 26 to 34).
2.  Select `app` from the run configuration dropdown in the top toolbar.
3.  Click the green **Run** button (Play icon, or press `Shift + F10`).
4.  The application will compile and install on your target device automatically.

#### Via Command Line (Gradle Wrapper):
Open a terminal in the project root directory and execute:
```powershell
# Compile the debug APK
.\gradlew assembleDebug

# Install and run on the connected device/emulator
.\gradlew installDebug
```
*The compiled APK file will be located at:*
`app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`

---

## 🔒 Permission Requirements

The application will prompt for the following system permissions as needed:
*   `android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS` — Required on Android 13+ (API 33+) to push task deadline alerts and Pomodoro completion notifications.
*   `android.permission.SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM` & `android.permission.USE_EXACT_ALARM` — Required to launch high-accuracy timer operations and exact calendar task alerts.
*   `android.permission.RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED` — Required to restore exact deadline alarms automatically after a device reboot.
*   `android.permission.VIBRATE` — Allows the Pomodoro timer to vibrate when sessions expire.
*   `android.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` (Max SDK version 28) — Required only on older versions of Android to save exported task PDFs into the shared `Documents` directory. On newer versions, it utilizes Scoped Storage (`MediaStore`) without requiring explicit permission.

########## ./app/src/androidTest/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/DaoTest.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertNull;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertTrue;

import android.content.Context;

import androidx.room.Room;
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4;
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry;

import org.junit.After;
import org.junit.Before;
import org.junit.Test;
import org.junit.runner.RunWith;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Instrumented DAO tests against an in-memory Room database (never touches
 * the real lorenz_flow_db on the device).
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4.class)
public class DaoTest {

    private AppDatabase db;

    @Before
    public void setUp() {
        Context context = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().getTargetContext();
        db = Room.inMemoryDatabaseBuilder(context, AppDatabase.class)
                .allowMainThreadQueries()
                .build();
    }

    @After
    public void tearDown() {
        db.close();
    }

    // ==================== helpers ====================

    private Task task(String title, long deadline, boolean completed, boolean archived) {
        Task t = new Task();
        t.setTitle(title);
        t.setDeadline(deadline);
        t.setCompleted(completed);
        t.setArchived(archived);
        return t;
    }

    private Note note(String title, int position, boolean archived) {
        Note n = new Note();
        n.setTitle(title);
        n.setBody("body");
        n.setPosition(position);
        n.setArchived(archived);
        return n;
    }

    private PasswordEntry password(String title, String username) {
        PasswordEntry entry = new PasswordEntry();
        entry.setTitle(title);
        entry.setUsername(username);
        entry.setEncryptedPassword("cipher");
        entry.setIv("iv");
        return entry;
    }

    // ==================== TaskDao ====================

    @Test
    public void getActiveTasks_excludesCompletedAndArchived_ordersDeadlineFirst() {
        db.taskDao().insert(task("plain", 0, false, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("deadline", 1, false, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("done", 0, true, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("archived", 0, false, true));

        List<Task> active = db.taskDao().getActiveTasks();

        assertEquals(2, active.size());
        // Tasks with a deadline sort before tasks without one
        assertEquals("deadline", active.get(0).getTitle());
        assertEquals("plain", active.get(1).getTitle());
    }

    @Test
    public void getCompletedTasks_returnsOnlyCompleted() {
        db.taskDao().insert(task("a", 0, true, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("b", 0, false, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("c", 0, true, true)); // archived+completed stays hidden

        List<Task> completed = db.taskDao().getCompletedTasks();

        assertEquals(1, completed.size());
        assertEquals("a", completed.get(0).getTitle());
    }

    @Test
    public void deleteAllCompleted_removesOnlyCompleted() {
        long doneId = db.taskDao().insert(task("done", 0, true, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("active", 0, false, false));

        db.taskDao().deleteAllCompleted();

        assertNull(db.taskDao().getTaskById(doneId));
        assertEquals(1, db.taskDao().getActiveTasks().size());
    }

    @Test
    public void archivedTasks_visibleOnlyInArchive_clearable() {
        db.taskDao().insert(task("archived", 0, false, true));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("active", 0, false, false));

        assertEquals(1, db.taskDao().getArchivedTasks().size());
        assertTrue(db.taskDao().getActiveTasksWithDeadline().isEmpty());

        db.taskDao().deleteAllArchived();

        assertTrue(db.taskDao().getArchivedTasks().isEmpty());
        assertEquals(1, db.taskDao().getActiveTasks().size());
    }

    @Test
    public void getActiveTasksWithDeadline_excludesNoDeadline() {
        db.taskDao().insert(task("with-deadline", 999, false, false));
        db.taskDao().insert(task("no-deadline", 0, false, false));

        List<Task> withDeadline = db.taskDao().getActiveTasksWithDeadline();

        assertEquals(1, withDeadline.size());
        assertEquals("with-deadline", withDeadline.get(0).getTitle());
    }

    @Test
    public void updateTask_roundtrips() {
        long id = db.taskDao().insert(task("before", 0, false, false));

        Task reloaded = db.taskDao().getTaskById(id);
        reloaded.setTitle("after");
        reloaded.setCompleted(true);
        db.taskDao().update(reloaded);

        Task after = db.taskDao().getTaskById(id);
        assertEquals("after", after.getTitle());
        assertTrue(after.isCompleted());
    }

    // ==================== NoteDao ====================

    @Test
    public void getAllNotes_excludesArchived_ordersByPosition() {
        db.noteDao().insert(note("second", 2, false));
        db.noteDao().insert(note("first", 0, false));
        db.noteDao().insert(note("gone", 1, true));

        List<Note> notes = db.noteDao().getAllNotes();

        assertEquals(2, notes.size());
        assertEquals("first", notes.get(0).getTitle()); // position 0
        assertEquals("second", notes.get(1).getTitle()); // position 2
    }

    @Test
    public void getArchivedNotes_returnsOnlyArchived() {
        db.noteDao().insert(note("kept", 0, false));
        db.noteDao().insert(note("archived", 0, true));

        List<Note> archived = db.noteDao().getArchivedNotes();

        assertEquals(1, archived.size());
        assertEquals("archived", archived.get(0).getTitle());
    }

    @Test
    public void noteById_roundtrips() {
        long id = db.noteDao().insert(note("find-me", 5, false));

        Note note = db.noteDao().getNoteById(id);
        assertEquals("find-me", note.getTitle());
        assertEquals(5, note.getPosition());
        assertFalse(note.isArchived());
    }

    // ==================== PasswordDao ====================

    @Test
    public void getAllPasswords_sortedByTitle() {
        db.passwordDao().insert(password("Zebra", "z"));
        db.passwordDao().insert(password("alpha", "a"));

        List<PasswordEntry> all = db.passwordDao().getAllPasswords();

        assertEquals(2, all.size());
        // SQLite default: case-sensitive ordering puts "Zebra" before "alpha"
        assertEquals("Zebra", all.get(0).getTitle());
        assertEquals("alpha", all.get(1).getTitle());
    }

    @Test
    public void passwordById_roundtrips() {
        long id = db.passwordDao().insert(password("Gmail", "user@example.com"));

        PasswordEntry entry = db.passwordDao().getPasswordById(id);
        assertEquals("Gmail", entry.getTitle());
        assertEquals("user@example.com", entry.getUsername());
        assertEquals("cipher", entry.getEncryptedPassword());
        assertEquals("iv", entry.getIv());
    }

    @Test
    public void deletePassword_removesIt() {
        long id = db.passwordDao().insert(password("Delete me", "u"));

        db.passwordDao().delete(db.passwordDao().getPasswordById(id));

        assertNull(db.passwordDao().getPasswordById(id));
        assertTrue(db.passwordDao().getAllPasswords().isEmpty());
    }
}


########## ./app/src/androidTest/java/com/lorenzflow/app/util/SecurityUtilTest.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.util;

import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertNull;

import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4;

import org.junit.Test;
import org.junit.runner.RunWith;

/**
 * Instrumented tests for SecurityUtil — crypto requires the AndroidKeyStore
 * provider and android.util.Base64, neither of which exist in plain JVM unit
 * tests, so these run on a device or emulator.
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4.class)
public class SecurityUtilTest {

    @Test
    public void encryptDecrypt_roundtrip_returnsOriginal() {
        String secret = "p@ssw0rd Ünïcode 123!";
        SecurityUtil.EncryptedData data = SecurityUtil.encrypt(secret);

        assertNotNull(data);
        assertNotNull(data.encryptedText);
        assertNotNull(data.iv);
        assertEquals(secret, SecurityUtil.decrypt(data.encryptedText, data.iv));
    }

    @Test
    public void encrypt_emptyString_roundtrips() {
        SecurityUtil.EncryptedData data = SecurityUtil.encrypt("");
        assertEquals("", SecurityUtil.decrypt(data.encryptedText, data.iv));
    }

    @Test
    public void encrypt_uniqueIvPerCall_sameInput() {
        SecurityUtil.EncryptedData first = SecurityUtil.encrypt("same input");
        SecurityUtil.EncryptedData second = SecurityUtil.encrypt("same input");

        assertNotEquals(first.iv, second.iv);
        assertNotEquals(first.encryptedText, second.encryptedText);
        assertEquals("same input", SecurityUtil.decrypt(first.encryptedText, first.iv));
        assertEquals("same input", SecurityUtil.decrypt(second.encryptedText, second.iv));
    }

    @Test
    public void decrypt_badIv_returnsNull_notGarbage() {
        SecurityUtil.EncryptedData data = SecurityUtil.encrypt("secret");
        assertNull(SecurityUtil.decrypt(data.encryptedText, "not-a-valid-iv"));
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml ##########
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">

    <!-- Notifications (API 33+) -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS" />

    <!-- Exact Alarms for task deadlines -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.USE_EXACT_ALARM" />

    <!-- Re-register alarms after device reboot -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED" />

    <!-- Vibration for timer alarms -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.VIBRATE" />

    <!-- PDF export to Documents (only needed for API < 29) -->
    <uses-permission
        android:name="android.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE"
        android:maxSdkVersion="28" />

    <application
        android:allowBackup="true"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:roundIcon="@mipmap/ic_launcher_round"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.LorenzFlow">

        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.LorenzFlow"
            android:windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <!-- Alarm receiver for task deadline notifications -->
        <receiver
            android:name=".notification.AlarmReceiver"
            android:exported="false" />

        <!-- Boot receiver to re-schedule alarms after reboot -->
        <receiver
            android:name=".notification.BootReceiver"
            android:directBootAware="false"
            android:exported="true">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.BOOT_COMPLETED" />
            </intent-filter>
        </receiver>

    </application>

</manifest>


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/MainActivity.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.MenuItem;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatDelegate;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.google.android.material.navigation.NavigationBarView;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ActivityMainBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.notification.NotificationHelper;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.AboutFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.CalendarFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.NotesFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.PomodoroFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.ProfileFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.SettingsFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.TasksFragment;

public class MainActivity extends AppCompatActivity {

    private ActivityMainBinding binding;

    @Override
    protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        // Load and apply theme preference before super.onCreate()
        SharedPreferences prefs = getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        int themeMode = prefs.getInt("pref_theme", 0); // 0 = System, 1 = Light, 2 = Dark
        if (themeMode == 1) {
            AppCompatDelegate.setDefaultNightMode(AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_NO);
        } else if (themeMode == 2) {
            AppCompatDelegate.setDefaultNightMode(AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_YES);
        } else {
            AppCompatDelegate.setDefaultNightMode(AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_FOLLOW_SYSTEM);
        }

        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
        binding = ActivityMainBinding.inflate(getLayoutInflater());
        setContentView(binding.getRoot());

        setSupportActionBar(binding.toolbar);

        binding.bottomNavigation.setOnItemSelectedListener(new NavigationBarView.OnItemSelectedListener() {
            @Override
            public boolean onNavigationItemSelected(@NonNull MenuItem item) {
                Fragment selectedFragment = null;
                int itemId = item.getItemId();
                if (itemId == R.id.nav_tasks) {
                    selectedFragment = new TasksFragment();
                } else if (itemId == R.id.nav_notes) {
                    selectedFragment = new NotesFragment();
                } else if (itemId == R.id.nav_calendar) {
                    selectedFragment = new CalendarFragment();
                } else if (itemId == R.id.nav_pomodoro) {
                    selectedFragment = new PomodoroFragment();
                } else if (itemId == R.id.nav_settings) {
                    selectedFragment = new SettingsFragment();
                }

                if (selectedFragment != null) {
                    updateNavIcons(itemId);
                    getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                            .replace(R.id.fragment_container, selectedFragment)
                            .commit();
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
        });

        // Set default fragment on first launch
        if (savedInstanceState == null) {
            binding.bottomNavigation.setSelectedItemId(R.id.nav_tasks);
        } else {
            // No state restore listener fires — sync the filled/outlined icons
            updateNavIcons(binding.bottomNavigation.getSelectedItemId());
        }

        checkNotificationPermission();
    }

    private final androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher<String> requestPermissionLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission(), isGranted -> {
                if (!isGranted) showNotificationsDeniedDialog();
            });

    /**
     * Called when POST_NOTIFICATIONS was denied. Explains why reminders matter
     * and offers a shortcut to the app's system notification settings.
     */
    private void showNotificationsDeniedDialog() {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(this)
                .setTitle(R.string.notifications_denied_title)
                .setMessage(R.string.notifications_denied_message)
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.open_notification_settings,
                        (dialog, which) -> NotificationHelper.openNotificationSettings(this))
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    /**
     * Swaps each tab's icon between filled (selected) and outlined
     * (unselected) variants. The Settings tab uses a single glyph.
     */
    private void updateNavIcons(int selectedId) {
        android.view.Menu menu = binding.bottomNavigation.getMenu();
        for (int i = 0; i < menu.size(); i++) {
            MenuItem item = menu.getItem(i);
            int id = item.getItemId();
            int iconRes;
            if (id == R.id.nav_tasks) {
                iconRes = id == selectedId
                        ? R.drawable.ic_nav_tasks_filled : R.drawable.ic_nav_tasks_outline;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_notes) {
                iconRes = id == selectedId
                        ? R.drawable.ic_nav_notes_filled : R.drawable.ic_nav_notes_outline;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_calendar) {
                iconRes = id == selectedId
                        ? R.drawable.ic_nav_calendar_filled : R.drawable.ic_nav_calendar_outline;
            } else if (id == R.id.nav_pomodoro) {
                iconRes = id == selectedId
                        ? R.drawable.ic_nav_timer_filled : R.drawable.ic_nav_timer_outline;
            } else {
                // Settings keeps one glyph; color + label weight mark the state
                continue;
            }
            item.setIcon(iconRes);
        }
    }

    private void checkNotificationPermission() {
        if (android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= android.os.Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            if (androidx.core.content.ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                    this, android.Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) !=
                    android.content.pm.PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                requestPermissionLauncher.launch(android.Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS);
            }
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/AppDatabase.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import android.content.Context;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.room.Database;
import androidx.room.Room;
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase;
import androidx.room.migration.Migration;
import androidx.sqlite.db.SupportSQLiteDatabase;

@Database(entities = {Task.class, Note.class, PasswordEntry.class}, version = 4, exportSchema = false)
public abstract class AppDatabase extends RoomDatabase {

    private static volatile AppDatabase INSTANCE;

    public abstract TaskDao taskDao();
    public abstract NoteDao noteDao();
    public abstract PasswordDao passwordDao();

    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Migrations — every schema change MUST have a migration listed here.
    // NEVER use fallbackToDestructiveMigration() after version 3; that wipes
    // all user data. Instead, add a new Migration block for each version bump.
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * v3 → v4: Added `archived` column (0 = active, 1 = archived) to both
     * `tasks` and `notes` tables. Default 0 keeps all existing rows visible.
     */
    static final Migration MIGRATION_3_4 = new Migration(3, 4) {
        @Override
        public void migrate(@NonNull SupportSQLiteDatabase database) {
            database.execSQL(
                "ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN archived INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0");
            database.execSQL(
                "ALTER TABLE notes ADD COLUMN archived INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0");
        }
    };

    // ─── Add new migrations above this line ───────────────────────────────────
    // Example for the next change:
    //
    // static final Migration MIGRATION_4_5 = new Migration(4, 5) {
    //     @Override
    //     public void migrate(@NonNull SupportSQLiteDatabase database) {
    //         database.execSQL("ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0");
    //     }
    // };
    //
    // Then add MIGRATION_4_5 to addMigrations(...) in getInstance() below.
    // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static AppDatabase getInstance(Context context) {
        if (INSTANCE == null) {
            synchronized (AppDatabase.class) {
                if (INSTANCE == null) {
                    INSTANCE = Room.databaseBuilder(
                            context.getApplicationContext(),
                            AppDatabase.class,
                            "lorenz_flow_db"
                    )
                    // Safe migrations — all user data is preserved.
                    .addMigrations(MIGRATION_3_4)
                    // Versions 1 and 2 were pre-release builds that used
                    // destructive migration themselves, so falling back on
                    // those is acceptable. Version 3+ is always migrated.
                    .fallbackToDestructiveMigrationFrom(1, 2)
                    .build();
                }
            }
        }
        return INSTANCE;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/Note.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import androidx.room.Entity;
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey;

@Entity(tableName = "notes")
public class Note {

    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true)
    private long id;

    private String title;
    private String body;
    private int color;          // color integer (0 = default)
    private long timestamp;
    private int position;
    private boolean archived;

    public Note() {
        this.timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    // Getters
    public long getId() { return id; }
    public String getTitle() { return title; }
    public String getBody() { return body; }
    public int getColor() { return color; }
    public long getTimestamp() { return timestamp; }
    public int getPosition() { return position; }
    public boolean isArchived() { return archived; }

    // Setters
    public void setId(long id) { this.id = id; }
    public void setTitle(String title) { this.title = title; }
    public void setBody(String body) { this.body = body; }
    public void setColor(int color) { this.color = color; }
    public void setTimestamp(long timestamp) { this.timestamp = timestamp; }
    public void setPosition(int position) { this.position = position; }
    public void setArchived(boolean archived) { this.archived = archived; }
}

package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import androidx.room.Dao;
import androidx.room.Delete;
import androidx.room.Insert;
import androidx.room.Query;
import androidx.room.Update;

import java.util.List;

@Dao
public interface NoteDao {

    @Insert
    long insert(Note note);

    @Update
    void update(Note note);

    @Delete
    void delete(Note note);

    @Query("SELECT * FROM notes WHERE archived = 0 ORDER BY position ASC, timestamp DESC")
    List<Note> getAllNotes();

    @Query("SELECT * FROM notes WHERE id = :id")
    Note getNoteById(long id);

    @Query("SELECT * FROM notes WHERE archived = 1 ORDER BY timestamp DESC")
    List<Note> getArchivedNotes();
}

########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/Task.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import androidx.room.Entity;
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey;

@Entity(tableName = "tasks")
public class Task {

    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true)
    private long id;

    private String title;
    private String description;
    private long deadline;      // epoch millis, 0 = no deadline
    private boolean completed;
    private long createdAt;
    private String category;
    private boolean archived; // e.g. "General", "Work", "Personal", "School", "Wellness", "Urgent"

    public Task() {
        this.createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
        this.category = "General"; // default category
    }

    // Getters
    public long getId() { return id; }
    public String getTitle() { return title; }
    public String getDescription() { return description; }
    public long getDeadline() { return deadline; }
    public boolean isCompleted() { return completed; }
    public long getCreatedAt() { return createdAt; }
    public String getCategory() { return category; }
    public boolean isArchived() { return archived; }

    // Setters
    public void setId(long id) { this.id = id; }
    public void setTitle(String title) { this.title = title; }
    public void setDescription(String description) { this.description = description; }
    public void setDeadline(long deadline) { this.deadline = deadline; }
    public void setCompleted(boolean completed) { this.completed = completed; }
    public void setCreatedAt(long createdAt) { this.createdAt = createdAt; }
    public void setCategory(String category) { this.category = category; }
    public void setArchived(boolean archived) { this.archived = archived; }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/TaskDao.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import androidx.room.Dao;
import androidx.room.Delete;
import androidx.room.Insert;
import androidx.room.Query;
import androidx.room.Update;

import java.util.List;

@Dao
public interface TaskDao {

    @Insert
    long insert(Task task);

    @Update
    void update(Task task);

    @Delete
    void delete(Task task);

    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE completed = 0 AND archived = 0 ORDER BY " +
           "CASE WHEN deadline > 0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, deadline ASC, createdAt DESC")
    List<Task> getActiveTasks();

    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE completed = 1 AND archived = 0 ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
    List<Task> getCompletedTasks();

    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE id = :id")
    Task getTaskById(long id);

    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE completed = 0 AND archived = 0 AND deadline > 0")
    List<Task> getActiveTasksWithDeadline();

    @Query("DELETE FROM tasks WHERE completed = 1 AND archived = 0")
    void deleteAllCompleted();

    @Query("SELECT * FROM tasks WHERE archived = 1 ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
    List<Task> getArchivedTasks();

    @Query("DELETE FROM tasks WHERE archived = 1")
    void deleteAllArchived();
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/notification/AlarmReceiver.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.notification;

import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;

/**
 * Receives alarm broadcasts at the task deadline time and fires a notification.
 */
public class AlarmReceiver extends BroadcastReceiver {

    public static final String EXTRA_TASK_ID = "extra_task_id";
    public static final String EXTRA_TASK_TITLE = "extra_task_title";

    @Override
    public void onReceive(Context context, Intent intent) {
        int taskId = intent.getIntExtra(EXTRA_TASK_ID, -1);
        String taskTitle = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TASK_TITLE);

        if (taskId == -1 || taskTitle == null) return;

        // Check if notifications are enabled in settings
        android.content.SharedPreferences prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        boolean notificationsEnabled = prefs.getBoolean("pref_notifications", true);
        if (!notificationsEnabled) return;

        NotificationHelper.showTaskNotification(context, taskId, taskTitle);
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/notification/BootReceiver.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.notification;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;

import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

/**
 * Re-schedules all pending task alarms after a device reboot.
 */
public class BootReceiver extends BroadcastReceiver {

    @Override
    public void onReceive(Context context, Intent intent) {
        if (intent == null || !Intent.ACTION_BOOT_COMPLETED.equals(intent.getAction())) {
            return;
        }

        // Re-register alarms on a background thread
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            AppDatabase db = AppDatabase.getInstance(context);
            List<Task> tasks = db.taskDao().getActiveTasksWithDeadline();
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

            for (Task task : tasks) {
                if (task.getDeadline() > now) {
                    scheduleAlarm(context, task);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private void scheduleAlarm(Context context, Task task) {
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (alarmManager == null) return;

        Intent alarmIntent = new Intent(context, AlarmReceiver.class);
        alarmIntent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_ID, (int) task.getId());
        alarmIntent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_TITLE, task.getTitle());

        PendingIntent pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                context,
                (int) task.getId(),
                alarmIntent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        // Try exact alarm, fall back to inexact if permission not granted
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            if (alarmManager.canScheduleExactAlarms()) {
                alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                        AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pendingIntent);
            } else {
                alarmManager.setAndAllowWhileIdle(
                        AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pendingIntent);
            }
        } else {
            alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                    AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pendingIntent);
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/notification/NotificationHelper.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.notification;

import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;

import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat;

import com.lorenzflow.app.MainActivity;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;

public class NotificationHelper {

    public static final String CHANNEL_ID = "Task_Deadlines";

    /**
     * Creates the notification channel. Safe to call multiple times;
     * the system ignores if the channel already exists.
     */
    public static void createNotificationChannel(Context context) {
        NotificationChannel channel = new NotificationChannel(
                CHANNEL_ID,
                context.getString(R.string.notification_channel_name),
                NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH
        );
        channel.setDescription(context.getString(R.string.notification_channel_desc));
        channel.enableVibration(true);
        channel.enableLights(true);

        NotificationManager manager = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager.class);
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.createNotificationChannel(channel);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Shows a deadline notification for the given task.
     *
     * @param context    Application context
     * @param taskId     Unique task ID used as notification ID
     * @param taskTitle  Title of the task to display
     */
    /**
     * Opens the system screen where the user can grant app notifications.
     * Safe to call on any API 26+ device (app minSdk).
     */
    public static void openNotificationSettings(Context context) {
        Intent intent = new Intent(android.provider.Settings.ACTION_APP_NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS)
                .putExtra(android.provider.Settings.EXTRA_APP_PACKAGE, context.getPackageName());
        try {
            context.startActivity(intent);
        } catch (android.content.ActivityNotFoundException | SecurityException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public static void showTaskNotification(Context context, int taskId, String taskTitle) {
        createNotificationChannel(context);

        Intent intent = new Intent(context, MainActivity.class);
        intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK);
        PendingIntent pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                context, taskId, intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        NotificationCompat.Builder builder = new NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL_ID)
                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_tasks)
                .setContentTitle(context.getString(R.string.app_name))
                .setContentText(context.getString(R.string.task_deadline_notification, taskTitle))
                .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_HIGH)
                .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_REMINDER)
                .setAutoCancel(true)
                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
                .setDefaults(NotificationCompat.DEFAULT_ALL);

        NotificationManager manager = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager.class);
        if (manager != null) {
            manager.notify(taskId, builder.build());
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/AboutFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.Intent;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentAboutBinding;

public class AboutFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentAboutBinding binding;

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentAboutBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        binding.rowFacebook.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            try {
                // Try to open the Facebook App
                Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("fb://facewebmodal/f?href=https://www.facebook.com/lorenshie/"));
                startActivity(intent);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                try {
                    // Fallback to browser
                    Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://www.facebook.com/lorenshie/"));
                    startActivity(intent);
                } catch (Exception ex) {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.no_browser_found, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            }
        });

        binding.rowEmail.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_SENDTO);
            intent.setData(Uri.parse("mailto:"));
            intent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_EMAIL, new String[]{"junlorenzmasangcay@gmail.com"});
            intent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, getString(R.string.feedback_subject));
            try {
                startActivity(intent);
            } catch (android.content.ActivityNotFoundException e) {
                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.no_email_client, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/ArchiveNotesFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.res.Configuration;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.StaggeredGridLayoutManager;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Note;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.NoteDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentArchiveNotesBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ItemNoteBinding;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class ArchiveNotesFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentArchiveNotesBinding binding;
    private NoteDao noteDao;
    private ArchivedNoteAdapter adapter;

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentArchiveNotesBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        noteDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).noteDao();

        // Back navigation
        binding.archiveNotesToolbar.setNavigationOnClickListener(v ->
                requireActivity().onBackPressed());

        // RecyclerView
        adapter = new ArchivedNoteAdapter();
        binding.recyclerArchivedNotes.setLayoutManager(
                new StaggeredGridLayoutManager(2, StaggeredGridLayoutManager.VERTICAL));
        binding.recyclerArchivedNotes.setAdapter(adapter);

        loadArchivedNotes();
    }

    private void loadArchivedNotes() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Note> archived = noteDao.getArchivedNotes();
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                adapter.setNotes(archived);
                boolean empty = archived.isEmpty();
                binding.archiveNotesEmptyState.setVisibility(empty ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
                binding.recyclerArchivedNotes.setVisibility(empty ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
            });
        });
    }

    private void showNoteOptions(Note note) {
        String label = (note.getTitle() != null && !note.getTitle().isEmpty())
                ? note.getTitle() : getString(R.string.this_note);
        String[] options = {getString(R.string.unarchive), getString(R.string.delete)};

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(label)
                .setItems(options, (dialog, which) -> {
                    if (which == 0) {
                        restoreNote(note);
                    } else {
                        confirmDeleteNote(note, label);
                    }
                })
                .show();
    }

    private void restoreNote(Note note) {
        note.setArchived(false);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            noteDao.update(note);
            if (getActivity() != null) {
                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.unarchive, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    loadArchivedNotes();
                });
            }
        });
    }

    private void confirmDeleteNote(Note note, String label) {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(R.string.delete)
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.permanently_delete_confirm, label))
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.delete, (d, w) ->
                        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                            noteDao.delete(note);
                            if (getActivity() != null) {
                                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.note_deleted,
                                            Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                                    loadArchivedNotes();
                                });
                            }
                        }))
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }

    // ===================== Inner Adapter =====================

    private class ArchivedNoteAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<ArchivedNoteAdapter.ViewHolder> {

        private List<Note> notes = new ArrayList<>();

        void setNotes(List<Note> notes) {
            this.notes = new ArrayList<>(notes);
            notifyDataSetChanged();
        }

        @NonNull
        @Override
        public ViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
            ItemNoteBinding b = ItemNoteBinding.inflate(
                    LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()), parent, false);
            return new ViewHolder(b);
        }

        @Override
        public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull ViewHolder holder, int position) {
            holder.bind(notes.get(position));
        }

        @Override
        public int getItemCount() {
            return notes.size();
        }

        class ViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
            private final ItemNoteBinding b;

            ViewHolder(ItemNoteBinding b) {
                super(b.getRoot());
                this.b = b;
            }

            void bind(Note note) {
                if (note.getTitle() != null && !note.getTitle().isEmpty()) {
                    b.textNoteTitle.setText(note.getTitle());
                    b.textNoteTitle.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                } else {
                    b.textNoteTitle.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                }
                b.textNoteBody.setText(note.getBody());

                // Card color — dimmed to indicate archived state
                boolean isDark = (b.getRoot().getContext().getResources().getConfiguration().uiMode
                        & Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK) == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES;
                int cardColor = NoteAdapter.getColorForIndex(note.getColor(), isDark);
                b.getRoot().setCardBackgroundColor(cardColor);
                b.getRoot().setAlpha(0.7f);

                // Tap → show Restore / Delete options
                b.getRoot().setOnClickListener(v -> showNoteOptions(note));
                b.getRoot().setOnLongClickListener(v -> {
                    showNoteOptions(note);
                    return true;
                });
            }
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/ArchiveTasksFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.TaskDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentArchiveTasksBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ItemArchivedTaskBinding;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class ArchiveTasksFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentArchiveTasksBinding binding;
    private TaskDao taskDao;
    private ArchivedTaskAdapter adapter;

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentArchiveTasksBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        taskDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).taskDao();

        // Back navigation via toolbar nav icon
        binding.archiveToolbar.setNavigationOnClickListener(v ->
                requireActivity().onBackPressed());

        // RecyclerView setup
        adapter = new ArchivedTaskAdapter();
        binding.recyclerArchivedTasks.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(requireContext()));
        binding.recyclerArchivedTasks.setAdapter(adapter);

        // Clear archive button
        binding.btnClearArchive.setOnClickListener(v -> confirmClearArchive());

        loadArchivedTasks();
    }

    private void loadArchivedTasks() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Task> archived = taskDao.getArchivedTasks();
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                adapter.setTasks(archived);
                boolean empty = archived.isEmpty();
                binding.archiveEmptyState.setVisibility(empty ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
                binding.recyclerArchivedTasks.setVisibility(empty ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
                binding.btnClearArchive.setVisibility(empty ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
            });
        });
    }

    private void restoreTask(Task task) {
        task.setArchived(false);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            taskDao.update(task);
            if (getActivity() != null) {
                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadArchivedTasks);
            }
        });
    }

    private void deleteArchivedTask(Task task) {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(R.string.delete)
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.permanently_delete_confirm, task.getTitle()))
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) ->
                        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                            taskDao.delete(task);
                            if (getActivity() != null) {
                                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadArchivedTasks);
                            }
                        }))
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    private void confirmClearArchive() {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(R.string.clear_archive)
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.delete_all_archived_tasks_confirm))
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.clear_archive, (dialog, which) ->
                        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                            taskDao.deleteAllArchived();
                            if (getActivity() != null) {
                                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadArchivedTasks);
                            }
                        }))
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }

    // ===================== Inner Adapter =====================

    private class ArchivedTaskAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<ArchivedTaskAdapter.ViewHolder> {

        private List<Task> tasks = new ArrayList<>();

        void setTasks(List<Task> tasks) {
            this.tasks = new ArrayList<>(tasks);
            notifyDataSetChanged();
        }

        @NonNull
        @Override
        public ViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
            ItemArchivedTaskBinding b = ItemArchivedTaskBinding.inflate(
                    LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()), parent, false);
            return new ViewHolder(b);
        }

        @Override
        public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull ViewHolder holder, int position) {
            holder.bind(tasks.get(position));
        }

        @Override
        public int getItemCount() {
            return tasks.size();
        }

        class ViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
            private final ItemArchivedTaskBinding b;

            ViewHolder(ItemArchivedTaskBinding b) {
                super(b.getRoot());
                this.b = b;
            }

            void bind(Task task) {
                b.textArchivedTaskTitle.setText(task.getTitle());

                if (task.getDescription() != null && !task.getDescription().isEmpty()) {
                    b.textArchivedTaskDescription.setText(task.getDescription());
                    b.textArchivedTaskDescription.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                } else {
                    b.textArchivedTaskDescription.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                }

                if (task.getCategory() != null && !task.getCategory().isEmpty()) {
                    b.textArchivedTaskCategory.setText(task.getCategory());
                    b.textArchivedTaskCategory.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);

                    int textColor;
                    int bgColor;
                    switch (task.getCategory()) {
                        case "Work":      textColor = 0xFF5856D6; bgColor = 0x1A5856D6; break;
                        case "Personal":  textColor = 0xFF34C759; bgColor = 0x1A34C759; break;
                        case "School":    textColor = 0xFFFF9500; bgColor = 0x1AFF9500; break;
                        case "Wellness":  textColor = 0xFF5AC8FA; bgColor = 0x1A5AC8FA; break;
                        case "Urgent":    textColor = 0xFFFF3B30; bgColor = 0x1AFF3B30; break;
                        default:          textColor = 0xFF8E8E93; bgColor = 0x1A8E8E93; break;
                    }
                    b.textArchivedTaskCategory.setTextColor(textColor);
                    b.textArchivedTaskCategory.getBackground().setTint(bgColor);
                } else {
                    b.textArchivedTaskCategory.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                }

                b.btnRestoreTask.setOnClickListener(v -> restoreTask(task));
                b.btnDeleteArchivedTask.setOnClickListener(v -> deleteArchivedTask(task));
            }
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/CalendarFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.DatePickerDialog;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.app.TimePickerDialog;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.google.android.material.snackbar.Snackbar;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.TaskDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.DialogAddTaskBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentCalendarBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.notification.AlarmReceiver;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Calendar;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class CalendarFragment extends Fragment implements TaskAdapter.OnTaskActionListener {

    private FragmentCalendarBinding binding;
    private TaskAdapter adapter;
    private TaskDao taskDao;

    private List<Task> allTasksWithDeadline = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Calendar selectedCalendar = Calendar.getInstance();

    private final SimpleDateFormat sdfHeader =
            new SimpleDateFormat("MMMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault());
    private final SimpleDateFormat sdfDialog =
            new SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy · hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault());

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentCalendarBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        taskDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).taskDao();

        setupRecyclerView();
        setupCalendarView();
        loadAllTasks();
    }

    private void setupRecyclerView() {
        adapter = new TaskAdapter(this);
        binding.recyclerCalendarTasks.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(requireContext()));
        binding.recyclerCalendarTasks.setAdapter(adapter);
    }

    private void setupCalendarView() {
        // Set initial header to today
        updateHeaderLabel();

        binding.calendarView.setOnDateChangeListener((calendarView, year, month, dayOfMonth) -> {
            selectedCalendar.set(Calendar.YEAR, year);
            selectedCalendar.set(Calendar.MONTH, month);
            selectedCalendar.set(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, dayOfMonth);

            updateHeaderLabel();
            filterTasksForSelectedDate();
        });
    }

    private void updateHeaderLabel() {
        String formattedDate = sdfHeader.format(selectedCalendar.getTime());
        binding.textSelectedDate.setText(getString(R.string.tasks_due_on, formattedDate));
    }

    private void loadAllTasks() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Task> tasks = taskDao.getActiveTasksWithDeadline();
            allTasksWithDeadline = tasks != null ? tasks : new ArrayList<>();

            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::filterTasksForSelectedDate);
        });
    }

    private void filterTasksForSelectedDate() {
        List<Task> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Task task : allTasksWithDeadline) {
            if (isSameDay(task.getDeadline(), selectedCalendar.getTimeInMillis())) {
                filtered.add(task);
            }
        }

        adapter.setTasks(filtered);

        boolean empty = filtered.isEmpty();
        binding.emptyStateCalendar.setVisibility(empty ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
        binding.recyclerCalendarTasks.setVisibility(empty ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
    }

    private boolean isSameDay(long time1, long time2) {
        Calendar cal1 = Calendar.getInstance();
        cal1.setTimeInMillis(time1);
        Calendar cal2 = Calendar.getInstance();
        cal2.setTimeInMillis(time2);
        return cal1.get(Calendar.YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.YEAR)
                && cal1.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == cal2.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR);
    }

    // ==================== TASK CALLBACKS ====================

    @Override
    public void onTaskCheckedChanged(Task task, boolean isChecked) {
        task.setCompleted(isChecked);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            taskDao.update(task);
            if (isChecked) {
                cancelAlarm(task);
            } else if (task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis()) {
                scheduleAlarm(task);
            }
            // Reload all tasks from database and refresh
            loadAllTasks();
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskClick(Task task) {
        showTaskDialog(task);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskDelete(Task task) {
        deleteTaskConfirm(task);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskShare(Task task) {
        Intent sendIntent = new Intent();
        sendIntent.setAction(Intent.ACTION_SEND);

        String shareContent = task.getTitle();
        if (task.getDescription() != null && !task.getDescription().isEmpty()) {
            shareContent += "\n\n" + task.getDescription();
        }
        if (task.getDeadline() > 0) {
            shareContent += "\n\nDeadline: " + sdfDialog.format(new Date(task.getDeadline()));
        }

        sendIntent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, shareContent);
        sendIntent.setType("text/plain");
        Intent shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, getString(R.string.transfer_task_to));
        startActivity(shareIntent);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskArchive(Task task) {
        task.setArchived(true);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            taskDao.update(task);
            cancelAlarm(task);
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                loadAllTasks();
                Snackbar.make(binding.getRoot(), R.string.task_archived, Snackbar.LENGTH_LONG)
                        .setAction(R.string.undo, v -> {
                            task.setArchived(false);
                            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                                taskDao.update(task);
                                if (task.getDeadline() > 0 && task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis() && !task.isCompleted()) {
                                    scheduleAlarm(task);
                                }
                                if (getActivity() != null) {
                                    requireActivity().runOnUiThread(CalendarFragment.this::loadAllTasks);
                                }
                            });
                        })
                        .show();
            });
        });
    }

    private void showTaskDialog(Task task) {
        DialogAddTaskBinding dialogBinding = DialogAddTaskBinding.inflate(getLayoutInflater());
        final long[] selectedDeadline = {task.getDeadline()};

        dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.setText(task.getTitle());
        dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.setText(task.getDescription());
        if (task.getDeadline() > 0) {
            dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setText(sdfDialog.format(new Date(task.getDeadline())));
        }

        // Pre-select category
        String category = task.getCategory();
        if (category != null) {
            switch (category) {
                case "Work":
                    dialogBinding.chipWork.setChecked(true);
                    break;
                case "Personal":
                    dialogBinding.chipPersonal.setChecked(true);
                    break;
                case "School":
                    dialogBinding.chipSchool.setChecked(true);
                    break;
                case "Wellness":
                    dialogBinding.chipWellness.setChecked(true);
                    break;
                case "Urgent":
                    dialogBinding.chipUrgent.setChecked(true);
                    break;
                case "General":
                default:
                    dialogBinding.chipGeneral.setChecked(true);
                    break;
            }
        }

        // Deadline picker
        dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
            if (selectedDeadline[0] > 0) {
                cal.setTimeInMillis(selectedDeadline[0]);
            }
            new DatePickerDialog(requireContext(), (view, year, month, day) -> {
                Calendar deadlineCal = Calendar.getInstance();
                deadlineCal.set(year, month, day);

                new TimePickerDialog(requireContext(), (timeView, hour, minute) -> {
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, hour);
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.MINUTE, minute);
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.SECOND, 0);
                    selectedDeadline[0] = deadlineCal.getTimeInMillis();
                    dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setText(sdfDialog.format(new Date(selectedDeadline[0])));
                }, cal.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY), cal.get(Calendar.MINUTE), false).show();
            }, cal.get(Calendar.YEAR), cal.get(Calendar.MONTH), cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)).show();
        });

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.edit_task))
                .setView(dialogBinding.getRoot())
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.save, (dialog, which) -> {
                    String title = dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String desc = dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.getText().toString().trim() : "";

                    if (title.isEmpty()) return;

                    // Get category
                    String selectedCat = "General";
                    int checkedChipId = dialogBinding.chipGroupCategory.getCheckedChipId();
                    if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_work) {
                        selectedCat = "Work";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_personal) {
                        selectedCat = "Personal";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_school) {
                        selectedCat = "School";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_wellness) {
                        selectedCat = "Wellness";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_urgent) {
                        selectedCat = "Urgent";
                    }

                    task.setTitle(title);
                    task.setDescription(desc);
                    task.setDeadline(selectedDeadline[0]);
                    task.setCategory(selectedCat);

                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        taskDao.update(task);
                        cancelAlarm(task);
                        if (task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis() && !task.isCompleted()) {
                            scheduleAlarm(task);
                        }
                        loadAllTasks();
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .setNeutralButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) -> deleteTaskConfirm(task))
                .show();
    }

    private void deleteTaskConfirm(Task task) {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(R.string.delete)
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.delete_task_confirm, task.getTitle()))
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) -> {
                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        taskDao.delete(task);
                        cancelAlarm(task);
                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.task_deleted, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                                loadAllTasks();
                            });
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    // ==================== ALARM MANAGEMENT ====================

    private void scheduleAlarm(Task task) {
        Context ctx = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (alarmManager == null) return;

        Intent intent = new Intent(ctx, AlarmReceiver.class);
        intent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_ID, (int) task.getId());
        intent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_TITLE, task.getTitle());

        PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                ctx, (int) task.getId(), intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            if (alarmManager.canScheduleExactAlarms()) {
                alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                        AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
            } else {
                alarmManager.setAndAllowWhileIdle(
                        AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
            }
        } else {
            alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                    AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
        }
    }

    private void cancelAlarm(Task task) {
        Context ctx = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (alarmManager == null) return;

        Intent intent = new Intent(ctx, AlarmReceiver.class);
        PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                ctx, (int) task.getId(), intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);
        alarmManager.cancel(pi);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/NoteAdapter.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.res.Configuration;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Note;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ItemNoteBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.AnimUtil;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
