public class NoteAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<NoteAdapter.NoteViewHolder> {

    public interface OnNoteClickListener {
        void onNoteClick(Note note);
        void onNoteLongClick(Note note);
        void onNoteArchive(Note note);
    }

    // Light mode note colors
    private static final int[] LIGHT_COLORS = {
            0xFFFFFBFF, // default
            0xFFFACDCD, // red
            0xFFFFE0B2, // orange
            0xFFFFF9C4, // yellow
            0xFFC8E6C9, // green
            0xFFB2DFDB, // teal
            0xFFBBDEFB, // blue
            0xFFE1BEE7  // purple
    };

    // Dark mode note colors
    private static final int[] DARK_COLORS = {
            0xFF2D2D30, // default
            0xFF5C2B29, // red
            0xFF614A19, // orange
            0xFF635D19, // yellow
            0xFF2D4A2E, // green
            0xFF16504B, // teal
            0xFF1E3A5F, // blue
            0xFF42275E  // purple
    };

    private List<Note> notes = new ArrayList<>();
    private final OnNoteClickListener listener;

    public NoteAdapter(OnNoteClickListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public void setNotes(List<Note> notes) {
        this.notes = new ArrayList<>(notes);
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    public List<Note> getNotes() {
        return notes;
    }

    /**
     * Returns the appropriate color array for the current theme mode.
     */
    public static int[] getColorPalette(boolean isDarkMode) {
        return isDarkMode ? DARK_COLORS : LIGHT_COLORS;
    }

    /**
     * Maps a color index (0-7) to the actual color integer.
     */
    public static int getColorForIndex(int index, boolean isDarkMode) {
        int[] palette = getColorPalette(isDarkMode);
        if (index >= 0 && index < palette.length) {
            return palette[index];
        }
        return palette[0];
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public NoteViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        ItemNoteBinding binding = ItemNoteBinding.inflate(
                LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()), parent, false);
        return new NoteViewHolder(binding);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull NoteViewHolder holder, int position) {
        holder.bind(notes.get(position));
        holder.fadeIn(position);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return notes.size();
    }

    class NoteViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final ItemNoteBinding binding;
        private boolean animatedIn;

        NoteViewHolder(ItemNoteBinding binding) {
            super(binding.getRoot());
            this.binding = binding;
        }

        /** Subtle fade-in, run once per ViewHolder instance. */
        void fadeIn(int position) {
            animatedIn = AnimUtil.fadeInOnce(binding.getRoot(), position, animatedIn);
        }

        void bind(Note note) {
            // Title
            if (note.getTitle() != null && !note.getTitle().isEmpty()) {
                binding.textNoteTitle.setText(note.getTitle());
                binding.textNoteTitle.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            } else {
                binding.textNoteTitle.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            // Body
            binding.textNoteBody.setText(note.getBody());

            // Card color
            boolean isDark = (binding.getRoot().getContext().getResources().getConfiguration().uiMode
                    & Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK) == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES;
            int cardColor = getColorForIndex(note.getColor(), isDark);
            binding.getRoot().setCardBackgroundColor(cardColor);

            // Click → edit
            binding.getRoot().setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onNoteClick(note);
            });

            // Long-press → options dialog (Archive or Delete)
            binding.getRoot().setOnLongClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) listener.onNoteLongClick(note);
                return true;
            });
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/NotesFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.content.res.Configuration;
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.Menu;
import android.view.MenuInflater;
import android.view.MenuItem;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.core.view.MenuProvider;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.StaggeredGridLayoutManager;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.google.android.material.snackbar.Snackbar;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Note;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.NoteDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.DialogAddNoteBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentNotesBinding;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class NotesFragment extends Fragment implements NoteAdapter.OnNoteClickListener {

    private FragmentNotesBinding binding;
    private NoteAdapter adapter;
    private NoteDao noteDao;

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentNotesBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        noteDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).noteDao();
        setupHeaderAvatar();

        adapter = new NoteAdapter(this);
        binding.recyclerNotes.setLayoutManager(
                new StaggeredGridLayoutManager(2, StaggeredGridLayoutManager.VERTICAL));
        binding.recyclerNotes.setAdapter(adapter);

        // Attach ItemTouchHelper for manual reordering
        androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper itemTouchHelper = new androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper(
                new androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper.SimpleCallback(
                        androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper.UP | androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper.DOWN |
                        androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper.LEFT | androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper.RIGHT, 0) {
            @Override
            public boolean onMove(@NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView recyclerView,
                                  @NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView.ViewHolder viewHolder,
                                  @NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView.ViewHolder target) {
                int fromPosition = viewHolder.getAdapterPosition();
                int toPosition = target.getAdapterPosition();

                List<Note> notes = adapter.getNotes();
                if (fromPosition < 0 || fromPosition >= notes.size() || toPosition < 0 || toPosition >= notes.size()) {
                    return false;
                }

                Note movedNote = notes.remove(fromPosition);
                notes.add(toPosition, movedNote);
                adapter.notifyItemMoved(fromPosition, toPosition);
                return true;
            }

            @Override
            public void onSwiped(@NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView.ViewHolder viewHolder, int direction) {
                // Swipe to dismiss not supported for notes
            }

            @Override
            public void clearView(@NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView recyclerView,
                                  @NonNull androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView.ViewHolder viewHolder) {
                super.clearView(recyclerView, viewHolder);
                // Save updated positions in background thread
                List<Note> notes = adapter.getNotes();
                Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                    for (int i = 0; i < notes.size(); i++) {
                        Note note = notes.get(i);
                        note.setPosition(i);
                        noteDao.update(note);
                    }
                });
            }
        });
        itemTouchHelper.attachToRecyclerView(binding.recyclerNotes);

        binding.fabAddNote.setOnClickListener(v -> showAddNoteDialog(null));

        // Toolbar menu for archive
        requireActivity().addMenuProvider(new MenuProvider() {
            @Override
            public void onCreateMenu(@NonNull Menu menu, @NonNull MenuInflater menuInflater) {
                menuInflater.inflate(R.menu.menu_notes, menu);
            }

            @Override
            public boolean onMenuItemSelected(@NonNull MenuItem menuItem) {
                if (menuItem.getItemId() == R.id.action_view_notes_archive) {
                    requireActivity().getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                            .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new ArchiveNotesFragment())
                            .addToBackStack(null)
                            .commit();
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
        }, getViewLifecycleOwner(), Lifecycle.State.RESUMED);

        loadNotes();
    }

    /** Shows the saved profile photo (same source as TasksFragment) or the
     *  default avatar when no photo was chosen yet. */
    private void setupHeaderAvatar() {
        Context ctx = requireContext();
        SharedPreferences prefs = ctx.getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        String imageUri = prefs.getString("profile_image_uri", null);
        if (imageUri != null && !imageUri.isEmpty()) {
            binding.avatarImage.setImageURI(Uri.parse(imageUri));
            if (binding.avatarImage.getDrawable() == null) {
                binding.avatarImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
            }
        } else {
            binding.avatarImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
        }
    }

    private void loadNotes() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Note> notes = noteDao.getAllNotes();
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                adapter.setNotes(notes);
                binding.emptyState.setVisibility(notes.isEmpty() ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
                binding.recyclerNotes.setVisibility(notes.isEmpty() ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
                updateScrollability();
            });
        });
    }

    private void updateScrollability() {
        if (binding == null) return;
        binding.recyclerNotes.getViewTreeObserver().addOnGlobalLayoutListener(
                new android.view.ViewTreeObserver.OnGlobalLayoutListener() {
                    @Override
                    public void onGlobalLayout() {
                        if (binding == null) return;
                        binding.recyclerNotes.getViewTreeObserver().removeOnGlobalLayoutListener(this);
                        boolean canScroll = binding.recyclerNotes.canScrollVertically(1)
                                || binding.recyclerNotes.canScrollVertically(-1);
                        if (binding.recyclerNotes.isNestedScrollingEnabled() != canScroll) {
                            binding.recyclerNotes.setNestedScrollingEnabled(canScroll);
                        }
                    }
                });
    }

    private boolean isDarkMode() {
        return (getResources().getConfiguration().uiMode & Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK)
                == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES;
    }

    /**
     * Shows the add/edit note dialog. If existingNote is not null, pre-fills fields for editing.
     */
    private void showAddNoteDialog(@Nullable Note existingNote) {
        DialogAddNoteBinding dialogBinding = DialogAddNoteBinding.inflate(getLayoutInflater());

        final int[] selectedColor = {existingNote != null ? existingNote.getColor() : 0};

        // Build color picker circles
        int[] palette = NoteAdapter.getColorPalette(isDarkMode());
        int circleSize = (int) (getResources().getDimension(R.dimen.color_circle_size));
        int margin = 6;

        for (int i = 0; i < palette.length; i++) {
            View circle = new View(requireContext());
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams params = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(circleSize, circleSize);
            params.setMargins(margin, margin, margin, margin);
            circle.setLayoutParams(params);

            GradientDrawable drawable = new GradientDrawable();
            drawable.setShape(GradientDrawable.OVAL);
            drawable.setColor(palette[i]);
            drawable.setStroke(selectedColor[0] == i ? 4 : 2,
                    isDarkMode() ? 0xFFC7C5D0 : 0xFF777680);
            circle.setBackground(drawable);

            final int colorIndex = i;
            circle.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                selectedColor[0] = colorIndex;
                // Update all circle strokes
                for (int j = 0; j < dialogBinding.colorPickerRow.getChildCount(); j++) {
                    View child = dialogBinding.colorPickerRow.getChildAt(j);
                    GradientDrawable bg = (GradientDrawable) child.getBackground();
                    bg.setStroke(j == colorIndex ? 4 : 2,
                            isDarkMode() ? 0xFFC7C5D0 : 0xFF777680);
                }
            });

            dialogBinding.colorPickerRow.addView(circle);
        }

        // Pre-fill if editing
        if (existingNote != null) {
            dialogBinding.editNoteTitle.setText(existingNote.getTitle());
            dialogBinding.editNoteBody.setText(existingNote.getBody());
        }

        String dialogTitle = existingNote != null ? getString(R.string.edit_note) : getString(R.string.add_note);

        MaterialAlertDialogBuilder builder = new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(dialogTitle)
                .setView(dialogBinding.getRoot())
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.save, (dialog, which) -> {
                    String title = dialogBinding.editNoteTitle.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editNoteTitle.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String body = dialogBinding.editNoteBody.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editNoteBody.getText().toString().trim() : "";

                    if (title.isEmpty() && body.isEmpty()) return;

                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        if (existingNote != null) {
                            existingNote.setTitle(title);
                            existingNote.setBody(body);
                            existingNote.setColor(selectedColor[0]);
                            noteDao.update(existingNote);
                        } else {
                            Note note = new Note();
                            note.setTitle(title);
                            note.setBody(body);
                            note.setColor(selectedColor[0]);
                            
                            // Make new note appear at the top
                            List<Note> existingNotes = noteDao.getAllNotes();
                            for (int i = 0; i < existingNotes.size(); i++) {
                                Note existing = existingNotes.get(i);
                                existing.setPosition(i + 1);
                                noteDao.update(existing);
                            }
                            
                            note.setPosition(0);
                            noteDao.insert(note);
                        }
                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadNotes);
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null);

        // Add delete button if editing
        if (existingNote != null) {
            builder.setNeutralButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) -> {
                Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                    noteDao.delete(existingNote);
                    if (getActivity() != null) {
                        requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.note_deleted, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                            loadNotes();
                        });
                    }
                });
            });
        }

        builder.show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onNoteClick(Note note) {
        showAddNoteDialog(note);
    }

    @Override
    public void onNoteLongClick(Note note) {
        // Show options: Archive or Delete
        String noteLabel = (note.getTitle() != null && !note.getTitle().isEmpty())
                ? note.getTitle() : getString(R.string.this_note);

        String[] options = {getString(R.string.archive), getString(R.string.delete)};

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(noteLabel)
                .setItems(options, (dialog, which) -> {
                    if (which == 0) {
                        // Archive
                        onNoteArchive(note);
                    } else {
                        // Delete
                        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                                .setTitle(R.string.delete)
                                .setMessage(getString(R.string.delete_note_confirm, noteLabel))
                                .setPositiveButton(R.string.delete, (d, w) -> {
                                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                                        noteDao.delete(note);
                                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                                                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.note_deleted,
                                                        Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                                                loadNotes();
                                            });
                                        }
                                    });
                                })
                                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                                .show();
                    }
                })
                .show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onNoteArchive(Note note) {
        note.setArchived(true);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            noteDao.update(note);
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                loadNotes();
                Snackbar.make(binding.getRoot(), R.string.note_archived, Snackbar.LENGTH_LONG)
                        .setAction(R.string.undo, v -> {
                            note.setArchived(false);
                            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                                noteDao.update(note);
                                if (getActivity() != null) {
                                    requireActivity().runOnUiThread(NotesFragment.this::loadNotes);
                                }
                            });
                        })
                        .show();
            });
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/PasswordAdapter.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Handler;
import android.os.Looper;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.PasswordEntry;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ItemPasswordBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.AnimUtil;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.SecurityUtil;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class PasswordAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<PasswordAdapter.PasswordViewHolder> {

    public interface OnPasswordActionListener {
        void onDeletePassword(PasswordEntry entry);
    }

    private List<PasswordEntry> allPasswords = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<PasswordEntry> displayedPasswords = new ArrayList<>();
    private final OnPasswordActionListener listener;

    // Set of password IDs currently set to visible (revealed or being revealed)
    private final Set<Long> visiblePasswordIds = new HashSet<>();
    // Short-lived cache of decrypted passwords; only holds rows the user
    // explicitly revealed, and is erased when the row is hidden again.
    private final Map<Long, String> decryptedCache = new HashMap<>();
    // Decrypt jobs already queued (prevents duplicate work on rapid rebinds)
    private final Set<Long> pendingDecrypts = new HashSet<>();
    private final Set<Long> failedDecrypts = new HashSet<>();

    // KeyStore/cipher calls are slow — never run them on the UI thread.
    private final ExecutorService cryptoExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> {
        Thread t = new Thread(r, "password-decrypt");
        t.setDaemon(true);
        return t;
    });
    private final Handler mainHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());

    public PasswordAdapter(OnPasswordActionListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public void setPasswords(List<PasswordEntry> passwords) {
        this.allPasswords = new ArrayList<>(passwords);
        this.displayedPasswords = new ArrayList<>(passwords);
        visiblePasswordIds.clear();
        decryptedCache.clear();
        pendingDecrypts.clear();
        failedDecrypts.clear();
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    public void filter(String query) {
        // Filtered rows keep any reveal/error state by entry ID on purpose
        displayedPasswords.clear();
        if (query.isEmpty()) {
            displayedPasswords.addAll(allPasswords);
        } else {
            String lowerQuery = query.toLowerCase().trim();
            for (PasswordEntry entry : allPasswords) {
                if (entry.getTitle().toLowerCase().contains(lowerQuery) ||
                        entry.getUsername().toLowerCase().contains(lowerQuery)) {
                    displayedPasswords.add(entry);
                }
            }
        }
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    /**
     * Decrypts on the crypto executor and reports the result on the main
     * thread. Does not touch any UI; callers decide what to do with the
     * decrypted value.
     */
    private void decryptInBackground(PasswordEntry entry, DecryptCallback callback) {
        cryptoExecutor.execute(() -> {
            String decrypted = SecurityUtil.decrypt(entry.getEncryptedPassword(), entry.getIv());
            mainHandler.post(() -> callback.onDecrypted(decrypted));
        });
    }

    private interface DecryptCallback {
        void onDecrypted(String decrypted);
    }

    /**
     * Reveal path: decrypt in background, then cache the result (if any) and
     * rebind just this row. Skips rows that already have a decrypt in flight.
     */
    private void scheduleRevealDecrypt(PasswordEntry entry, long entryId) {
        if (!pendingDecrypts.add(entryId)) return;

        cryptoExecutor.execute(() -> {
            String decrypted = SecurityUtil.decrypt(entry.getEncryptedPassword(), entry.getIv());
            mainHandler.post(() -> {
                pendingDecrypts.remove(entryId);
                if (decrypted != null) {
                    decryptedCache.put(entryId, decrypted);
                    failedDecrypts.remove(entryId);
                } else {
                    failedDecrypts.add(entryId);
                }
                int position = displayedPasswords.indexOf(entry);
                if (position >= 0) {
                    notifyItemChanged(position);
                }
            });
        });
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public PasswordViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        ItemPasswordBinding binding = ItemPasswordBinding.inflate(
                LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()), parent, false);
        return new PasswordViewHolder(binding);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull PasswordViewHolder holder, int position) {
        holder.bind(displayedPasswords.get(position));
        holder.fadeIn(position);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return displayedPasswords.size();
    }

    class PasswordViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final ItemPasswordBinding binding;
        private boolean animatedIn;

        PasswordViewHolder(ItemPasswordBinding binding) {
            super(binding.getRoot());
            this.binding = binding;
        }

        /** Subtle fade-in, run once per ViewHolder instance. */
        void fadeIn(int position) {
            animatedIn = AnimUtil.fadeInOnce(binding.getRoot(), position, animatedIn);
        }

        void bind(PasswordEntry entry) {
            binding.textPasswordTitle.setText(entry.getTitle());
            binding.textPasswordUsername.setText(entry.getUsername());

            Context ctx = binding.getRoot().getContext();
            long entryId = entry.getId();
            boolean isVisible = visiblePasswordIds.contains(entryId);

            if (isVisible) {
                String cached = decryptedCache.get(entryId);
                if (cached != null) {
                    // Fast path: already decrypted, no re-decryption on rebind
                    binding.textPasswordVal.setText(cached);
                    binding.btnToggleVisibility.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_expand_less);
                } else if (failedDecrypts.contains(entryId)) {
                    binding.textPasswordVal.setText(ctx.getString(R.string.decrypt_error));
                    binding.btnToggleVisibility.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_visibility);
                } else {
                    // Decrypting in background; show dots until the row rebinds
                    binding.textPasswordVal.setText("••••••••••••");
                    binding.btnToggleVisibility.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_visibility);
                    scheduleRevealDecrypt(entry, entryId);
                }
            } else {
                binding.textPasswordVal.setText("••••••••••••");
                binding.btnToggleVisibility.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_visibility);
            }

            // Copy username
            binding.btnCopyUsername.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                ClipboardManager clipboard = (ClipboardManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                ClipData clip = ClipData.newPlainText("username", entry.getUsername());
                if (clipboard != null) {
                    clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip);
                    Toast.makeText(ctx, R.string.username_copied, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            });

            // Copy password — decrypt happens off the UI thread
            binding.btnCopyPassword.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                decryptInBackground(entry, decrypted -> {
                    if (decrypted == null) {
                        Toast.makeText(ctx, R.string.error_decrypting_password, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        return;
                    }
                    ClipboardManager clipboard = (ClipboardManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                    ClipData clip = ClipData.newPlainText("password", decrypted);
                    if (clipboard != null) {
                        clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip);
                        Toast.makeText(ctx, R.string.password_copied, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    }
                });
            });

            // Toggle visibility click — reveal work happens on the next bind()
            binding.btnToggleVisibility.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                int adapterPosition = getAdapterPosition();
                if (adapterPosition == RecyclerView.NO_POSITION) return;

                if (visiblePasswordIds.contains(entryId)) {
                    visiblePasswordIds.remove(entryId);
                    decryptedCache.remove(entryId); // keep plaintext ephemeral
                    failedDecrypts.remove(entryId);
                } else {
                    visiblePasswordIds.add(entryId);
                    decryptedCache.remove(entryId);
                }
                notifyItemChanged(adapterPosition);
            });

            // Delete click
            binding.btnDeletePassword.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onDeletePassword(entry);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/PasswordManagerFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.KeyguardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.text.Editable;
import android.text.TextWatcher;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher;
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts;
import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.PasswordDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.PasswordEntry;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.DialogAddPasswordBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentPasswordManagerBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.SecurityUtil;

import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class PasswordManagerFragment extends Fragment implements PasswordAdapter.OnPasswordActionListener {

    private FragmentPasswordManagerBinding binding;
    private PasswordAdapter adapter;
    private PasswordDao passwordDao;
    private SharedPreferences prefs;
    private boolean isAuthenticated = false;

    private final ActivityResultLauncher<Intent> authLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
            new ActivityResultContracts.StartActivityForResult(),
            result -> {
                if (result.getResultCode() == Activity.RESULT_OK) {
                    isAuthenticated = true;
                    loadPasswords();
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.auth_failed, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack();
                }
            });

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentPasswordManagerBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        passwordDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).passwordDao();
        prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);

        setupRecyclerView();
        setupSearch();
        setupFab();

        if (!isAuthenticated) {
            authenticateUser();
        } else {
            loadPasswords();
        }
    }

    private void setupRecyclerView() {
        adapter = new PasswordAdapter(this);
        binding.recyclerPasswords.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(requireContext()));
        binding.recyclerPasswords.setAdapter(adapter);
    }

    private void setupSearch() {
        binding.editSearchPasswords.addTextChangedListener(new TextWatcher() {
            @Override
            public void beforeTextChanged(CharSequence s, int start, int count, int after) {}

            @Override
            public void onTextChanged(CharSequence s, int start, int before, int count) {
                adapter.filter(s.toString());
            }

            @Override
            public void afterTextChanged(Editable s) {}
        });
    }

    private void setupFab() {
        binding.fabAddPassword.setOnClickListener(v -> showAddPasswordDialog());
    }

    private void authenticateUser() {
        KeyguardManager km = (KeyguardManager) requireContext().getSystemService(Context.KEYGUARD_SERVICE);
        if (km != null && km.isDeviceSecure()) {
            Intent authIntent = km.createConfirmDeviceCredentialIntent(
                    getString(R.string.pm_lock_title), getString(R.string.pm_lock_desc));
            if (authIntent != null) {
                authLauncher.launch(authIntent);
            } else {
                checkCustomAppPin();
            }
        } else {
            checkCustomAppPin();
        }
    }

    private void checkCustomAppPin() {
        String savedPinHash = prefs.getString("pm_master_pin_hash", "");
        if (savedPinHash.isEmpty()) {
            // Setup master PIN
            showSetupPinDialog();
        } else {
            // Enter master PIN
            showEnterPinDialog(savedPinHash);
        }
    }

    private void showSetupPinDialog() {
        android.view.View dialogView = getLayoutInflater().inflate(R.layout.dialog_master_pin, null);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputLayout layoutMasterPin =
                dialogView.findViewById(R.id.layout_master_pin);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText editPin =
                dialogView.findViewById(R.id.edit_master_pin);

        layoutMasterPin.setHint(getString(R.string.pm_pin_hint_setup));

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.secure_setup_title))
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.secure_setup_message))
                .setView(dialogView)
                .setCancelable(false)
                .setPositiveButton(getString(R.string.set_pin), (dialog, which) -> {
                    String pin = editPin.getText() != null ? editPin.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    if (pin.length() < 4) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.pin_too_short, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack();
                        return;
                    }
                    String hash = hashString(pin);
                    if (hash != null) {
                        prefs.edit().putString("pm_master_pin_hash", hash).apply();
                        isAuthenticated = true;
                        loadPasswords();
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.pin_set_success, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    } else {
                        getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack();
                    }
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, (dialog, which) -> getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack())
                .show();
    }

    private void showEnterPinDialog(String savedHash) {
        android.view.View dialogView = getLayoutInflater().inflate(R.layout.dialog_master_pin, null);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputLayout layoutMasterPin =
                dialogView.findViewById(R.id.layout_master_pin);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText editPin =
                dialogView.findViewById(R.id.edit_master_pin);

        layoutMasterPin.setHint(getString(R.string.pm_pin_hint_enter));

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.unlock_password_manager))
                .setView(dialogView)
                .setCancelable(false)
                .setPositiveButton(getString(R.string.unlock), (dialog, which) -> {
                    String pin = editPin.getText() != null ? editPin.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String hash = hashString(pin);
                    if (hash != null && hash.equals(savedHash)) {
                        isAuthenticated = true;
                        loadPasswords();
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.incorrect_pin, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack();
                    }
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, (dialog, which) -> getParentFragmentManager().popBackStack())
                .show();
    }

    private String hashString(String input) {
        try {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] hash = digest.digest(input.getBytes("UTF-8"));
            StringBuilder hexString = new StringBuilder();
            for (byte b : hash) {
                String hex = Integer.toHexString(0xff & b);
                if (hex.length() == 1) hexString.append('0');
                hexString.append(hex);
            }
            return hexString.toString();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return null;
        }
    }

    private void loadPasswords() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<PasswordEntry> list = passwordDao.getAllPasswords();
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                adapter.setPasswords(list);
                binding.emptyStatePasswords.setVisibility(list.isEmpty() ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
                binding.recyclerPasswords.setVisibility(list.isEmpty() ? View.GONE : View.VISIBLE);
            });
        });
    }

    private void showAddPasswordDialog() {
        DialogAddPasswordBinding dialogBinding = DialogAddPasswordBinding.inflate(getLayoutInflater());

        dialogBinding.btnGeneratePassword.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            String securePass = generateSecurePassword();
            dialogBinding.editPasswordVal.setText(securePass);
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.secure_password_generated, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        });

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.add_credential))
                .setView(dialogBinding.getRoot())
                .setPositiveButton("Save", (dialog, which) -> {
                    String title = dialogBinding.editPasswordTitle.getText() != null ? dialogBinding.editPasswordTitle.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String username = dialogBinding.editPasswordUsername.getText() != null ? dialogBinding.editPasswordUsername.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String password = dialogBinding.editPasswordVal.getText() != null ? dialogBinding.editPasswordVal.getText().toString().trim() : "";

                    if (title.isEmpty() || username.isEmpty() || password.isEmpty()) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.pm_fields_required, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        return;
                    }

                    SecurityUtil.EncryptedData encrypted = SecurityUtil.encrypt(password);
                    if (encrypted != null) {
                        PasswordEntry entry = new PasswordEntry();
                        entry.setTitle(title);
                        entry.setUsername(username);
                        entry.setEncryptedPassword(encrypted.encryptedText);
                        entry.setIv(encrypted.iv);

                        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                            passwordDao.insert(entry);
                            if (getActivity() != null) {
                                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadPasswords);
                            }
                        });
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.error_encrypting_password, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    }
                })
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    private String generateSecurePassword() {
        String upper = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ";
        String lower = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
        String digits = "0123456789";
        String punctuation = "!@#$%^&*()_-+=<>?";
        String all = upper + lower + digits + punctuation;
        SecureRandom random = new SecureRandom();
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        
        sb.append(upper.charAt(random.nextInt(upper.length())));
        sb.append(lower.charAt(random.nextInt(lower.length())));
        sb.append(digits.charAt(random.nextInt(digits.length())));
        sb.append(punctuation.charAt(random.nextInt(punctuation.length())));
        
        for (int i = 4; i < 16; i++) {
            sb.append(all.charAt(random.nextInt(all.length())));
        }
        
        char[] array = sb.toString().toCharArray();
        for (int i = array.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
            int index = random.nextInt(i + 1);
            char a = array[index];
            array[index] = array[i];
            array[i] = a;
        }
        return new String(array);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDeletePassword(PasswordEntry entry) {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.pm_delete_password_title))
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.pm_delete_password_confirm, entry.getTitle()))
                .setPositiveButton(getString(R.string.delete), (dialog, which) -> {
                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        passwordDao.delete(entry);
                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadPasswords);
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/PomodoroFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.app.NotificationChannel;
import android.app.NotificationManager;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.media.Ringtone;
import android.media.RingtoneManager;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.os.CountDownTimer;
import android.os.VibrationEffect;
import android.os.Vibrator;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.lorenzflow.app.MainActivity;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentPomodoroBinding;

import java.util.Locale;

public class PomodoroFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentPomodoroBinding binding;

    private long focusTimeMillis;
    private long shortBreakTimeMillis;
    private long longBreakTimeMillis;

    private long totalTimeMillis;
    private long timeLeftMillis;

    private CountDownTimer countDownTimer;
    private boolean isTimerRunning = false;
    private String currentMode = "Focus"; // "Focus", "Short", "Long"

    private Ringtone activeRingtone;
    private Vibrator vibrator;

    private static final String CHANNEL_ID = "Pomodoro_Timer";

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentPomodoroBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        vibrator = (Vibrator) requireContext().getSystemService(Context.VIBRATOR_SERVICE);

        loadDurations();
        timeLeftMillis = totalTimeMillis;

        setupModeSelection();
        setupControls();
        updateTimerText();
        updateProgressBar();
    }

    private void loadDurations() {
        if (getContext() == null) return;
        android.content.SharedPreferences prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        focusTimeMillis = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_focus_mins", 25) * 60 * 1000L;
        shortBreakTimeMillis = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_short_mins", 5) * 60 * 1000L;
        longBreakTimeMillis = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_long_mins", 15) * 60 * 1000L;

        if (currentMode.equals("Focus")) {
            totalTimeMillis = focusTimeMillis;
        } else if (currentMode.equals("Short")) {
            totalTimeMillis = shortBreakTimeMillis;
        } else {
            totalTimeMillis = longBreakTimeMillis;
        }
    }

    private void setupModeSelection() {
        binding.toggleGroupModes.addOnButtonCheckedListener((group, checkedId, isChecked) -> {
            if (!isChecked) return;

            // Stop existing timer
            if (isTimerRunning) {
                pauseTimer();
            }

            if (checkedId == R.id.btn_mode_focus) {
                currentMode = "Focus";
                totalTimeMillis = focusTimeMillis;
                binding.textStatus.setText(getString(R.string.pomodoro_status_focus));
                binding.pomodoroProgress.setIndicatorColor(requireContext().getColor(R.color.lf_accent));
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.btn_mode_short) {
                currentMode = "Short";
                totalTimeMillis = shortBreakTimeMillis;
                binding.textStatus.setText(getString(R.string.pomodoro_status_short));
                binding.pomodoroProgress.setIndicatorColor(requireContext().getColor(R.color.lf_pastel_mint_deep));
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.btn_mode_long) {
                currentMode = "Long";
                totalTimeMillis = longBreakTimeMillis;
                binding.textStatus.setText(getString(R.string.pomodoro_status_long));
                binding.pomodoroProgress.setIndicatorColor(requireContext().getColor(R.color.lf_pastel_lilac_deep));
            }

            timeLeftMillis = totalTimeMillis;
            updateTimerText();
            updateProgressBar();
            binding.btnStartPause.setText(getString(R.string.start));
        });
    }

    private void setupControls() {
        binding.btnStartPause.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            if (isTimerRunning) {
                pauseTimer();
            } else {
                startTimer();
            }
        });

        binding.btnReset.setOnClickListener(v -> resetTimer());

        binding.btnChangeDurations.setOnClickListener(v -> showEditDurationsDialog());
    }

    private void showEditDurationsDialog() {
        android.content.SharedPreferences prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);
        int currentFocus = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_focus_mins", 25);
        int currentShort = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_short_mins", 5);
        int currentLong = prefs.getInt("pomodoro_long_mins", 15);

        android.view.View dialogView = getLayoutInflater().inflate(R.layout.dialog_edit_durations, null);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText editFocus = dialogView.findViewById(R.id.edit_focus_mins);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText editShort = dialogView.findViewById(R.id.edit_short_mins);
        com.google.android.material.textfield.TextInputEditText editLong = dialogView.findViewById(R.id.edit_long_mins);

        editFocus.setText(String.valueOf(currentFocus));
        editShort.setText(String.valueOf(currentShort));
        editLong.setText(String.valueOf(currentLong));

        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.adjust_durations))
                .setView(dialogView)
                .setPositiveButton("Save", (dialog, which) -> {
                    String focusStr = editFocus.getText() != null ? editFocus.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String shortStr = editShort.getText() != null ? editShort.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String longStr = editLong.getText() != null ? editLong.getText().toString().trim() : "";

                    if (focusStr.isEmpty() || shortStr.isEmpty() || longStr.isEmpty()) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.durations_empty, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                        return;
                    }

                    try {
                        int newFocus = Integer.parseInt(focusStr);
                        int newShort = Integer.parseInt(shortStr);
                        int newLong = Integer.parseInt(longStr);

                        if (newFocus <= 0 || newShort <= 0 || newLong <= 0) {
                            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.durations_positive, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                            return;
                        }

                        prefs.edit()
                                .putInt("pomodoro_focus_mins", newFocus)
                                .putInt("pomodoro_short_mins", newShort)
                                .putInt("pomodoro_long_mins", newLong)
                                .apply();

                        // Pause and reload
                        pauseTimer();
                        loadDurations();
                        resetTimer();
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.durations_updated, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();

                    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.invalid_number_format, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                    }
                })
                .setNegativeButton("Cancel", null)
                .show();
    }

    private void startTimer() {
        countDownTimer = new CountDownTimer(timeLeftMillis, 100) {
            @Override
            public void onTick(long millisUntilFinished) {
                timeLeftMillis = millisUntilFinished;
                updateTimerText();
                updateProgressBar();
            }

            @Override
            public void onFinish() {
                isTimerRunning = false;
                timeLeftMillis = 0;
                updateTimerText();
                updateProgressBar();
                binding.btnStartPause.setText(getString(R.string.start));
                triggerAlarm();
            }
        }.start();

        isTimerRunning = true;
        binding.btnStartPause.setText(getString(R.string.pause));
    }

    private void pauseTimer() {
        if (countDownTimer != null) {
            countDownTimer.cancel();
        }
        isTimerRunning = false;
        binding.btnStartPause.setText(getString(R.string.start));
    }

    private void resetTimer() {
        pauseTimer();
        timeLeftMillis = totalTimeMillis;
        updateTimerText();
        updateProgressBar();
    }

    private void updateTimerText() {
        int minutes = (int) (timeLeftMillis / 1000) / 60;
        int seconds = (int) (timeLeftMillis / 1000) % 60;
        String timeStr = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%02d:%02d", minutes, seconds);
        binding.textTimer.setText(timeStr);
    }

    private void updateProgressBar() {
        int progress = (int) ((timeLeftMillis * 100) / totalTimeMillis);
        binding.pomodoroProgress.setProgress(progress);
    }

    private void triggerAlarm() {
        // 1. Play sound
        try {
            Uri alert = RingtoneManager.getDefaultUri(RingtoneManager.TYPE_ALARM);
            if (alert == null) {
                alert = RingtoneManager.getDefaultUri(RingtoneManager.TYPE_NOTIFICATION);
            }
            if (alert != null) {
                activeRingtone = RingtoneManager.getRingtone(requireContext(), alert);
                if (activeRingtone != null) {
                    activeRingtone.play();
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // 2. Vibrate
        if (vibrator != null && vibrator.hasVibrator()) {
            long[] pattern = {0, 500, 500, 500, 500};
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                vibrator.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createWaveform(pattern, 0));
            } else {
                vibrator.vibrate(pattern, 0);
            }
        }

        // 3. Show notification
        sendTimerNotification();

        // 4. Show alert dialog to turn off the alarm
        String title = getString(R.string.timer_finished);
        String message = currentMode.equals("Focus") ? getString(R.string.take_a_break) : getString(R.string.time_to_focus);

        if (getActivity() != null) {
            new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireActivity())
                    .setTitle(title)
                    .setMessage(message)
                    .setCancelable(false)
                    .setPositiveButton("OK", (dialog, which) -> stopAlarm())
                    .show();
        }

        // Automatically stop alarm after 15 seconds to prevent continuous ringing
        binding.getRoot().postDelayed(this::stopAlarm, 15000);
    }

    private void stopAlarm() {
        if (activeRingtone != null && activeRingtone.isPlaying()) {
            activeRingtone.stop();
        }
        if (vibrator != null) {
            vibrator.cancel();
        }
    }

    private void sendTimerNotification() {
        Context context = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        NotificationManager manager = (NotificationManager) context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE);
        if (manager == null) return;

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            NotificationChannel channel = new NotificationChannel(
                    CHANNEL_ID,
                    getString(R.string.pomodoro_notification_title),
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH
            );
            channel.enableVibration(true);
            channel.enableLights(true);
            manager.createNotificationChannel(channel);
        }

        Intent intent = new Intent(context, MainActivity.class);
        intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK | Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK);
        PendingIntent pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                context, 999, intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        );

        String message = currentMode.equals("Focus") ? getString(R.string.pomodoro_done_focus) : getString(R.string.pomodoro_done_break);

        NotificationCompat.Builder builder = new NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL_ID)
                .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_timer)
                .setContentTitle(context.getString(R.string.pomodoro_notification_title))
                .setContentText(message)
                .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_HIGH)
                .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_ALARM)
                .setAutoCancel(true)
                .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
                .setDefaults(NotificationCompat.DEFAULT_ALL);

        manager.notify(999, builder.build());
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        pauseTimer();
        stopAlarm();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/ProfileFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher;
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts;
import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentProfileBinding;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.InputStream;

public class ProfileFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentProfileBinding binding;
    private SharedPreferences prefs;

    private static final String PREFS_NAME = "lorenz_flow_prefs";
    private static final String PREF_NAME_KEY = "profile_name";
    private static final String PREF_BIO_KEY = "profile_bio";
    private static final String PREF_IMAGE_URI_KEY = "profile_image_uri";

    private final ActivityResultLauncher<String> getContentLauncher =
            registerForActivityResult(new ActivityResultContracts.GetContent(), uri -> {
                if (uri != null) {
                    saveImageLocally(uri);
                }
            });

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentProfileBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);

        loadProfileData();

        binding.btnChangePhoto.setOnClickListener(v -> getContentLauncher.launch("image/*"));

        binding.btnSaveProfile.setOnClickListener(v -> saveProfileData());
    }

    private void loadProfileData() {
        String name = prefs.getString(PREF_NAME_KEY, "");
        String bio = prefs.getString(PREF_BIO_KEY, "");
        String imageUriStr = prefs.getString(PREF_IMAGE_URI_KEY, null);

        binding.editName.setText(name);
        binding.editBio.setText(bio);

        if (imageUriStr != null) {
            try {
                binding.profileImage.setImageURI(Uri.parse(imageUriStr));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                binding.profileImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
            }
        } else {
            binding.profileImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
        }
    }

    private void saveProfileData() {
        String name = binding.editName.getText() != null ? binding.editName.getText().toString().trim() : "";
        String bio = binding.editBio.getText() != null ? binding.editBio.getText().toString().trim() : "";

        SharedPreferences.Editor editor = prefs.edit();
        editor.putString(PREF_NAME_KEY, name);
        editor.putString(PREF_BIO_KEY, bio);
        editor.apply();

        Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.profile_saved, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
    }

    private void saveImageLocally(Uri imageUri) {
        try {
            InputStream is = requireContext().getContentResolver().openInputStream(imageUri);
            if (is == null) return;

            File file = new File(requireContext().getFilesDir(), "profile_pic.jpg");
            FileOutputStream os = new FileOutputStream(file);

            byte[] buffer = new byte[1024];
            int bytesRead;
            while ((bytesRead = is.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                os.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
            }

            is.close();
            os.close();

            String savedUriStr = Uri.fromFile(file).toString();
            prefs.edit().putString(PREF_IMAGE_URI_KEY, savedUriStr).apply();

            binding.profileImage.setImageURI(Uri.parse(savedUriStr));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.failed_to_save_profile_picture, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/SettingsFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.SharedPreferences;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.RadioGroup;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatDelegate;
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentSettingsBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.notification.NotificationHelper;

public class SettingsFragment extends Fragment {

    private FragmentSettingsBinding binding;
    private SharedPreferences prefs;
    private android.widget.CompoundButton.OnCheckedChangeListener notifToggleListener;

    private static final String PREFS_NAME = "lorenz_flow_prefs";
    private static final String PREF_THEME_KEY = "pref_theme"; // 0 = System, 1 = Light, 2 = Dark
    private static final String PREF_NOTIF_KEY = "pref_notifications";

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentSettingsBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE);

        setupThemeSelection();
        setupNotificationSelection();
        setupNavigationRows();
    }

    private void setupNavigationRows() {
        binding.rowProfileSettings.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            getParentFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                    .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new ProfileFragment())
                    .addToBackStack(null)
                    .commit();
        });

        binding.rowPasswordManager.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            getParentFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                    .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new PasswordManagerFragment())
                    .addToBackStack(null)
                    .commit();
        });

        binding.rowAboutSettings.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            getParentFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                    .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new AboutFragment())
                    .addToBackStack(null)
                    .commit();
        });
    }

    private void setupThemeSelection() {
        int savedTheme = prefs.getInt(PREF_THEME_KEY, 0); // Default: 0 (System)

        // Select the active theme radio button
        if (savedTheme == 1) {
            binding.radioThemeLight.setChecked(true);
        } else if (savedTheme == 2) {
            binding.radioThemeDark.setChecked(true);
        } else {
            binding.radioThemeSystem.setChecked(true);
        }

        binding.radioGroupTheme.setOnCheckedChangeListener((group, checkedId) -> {
            int selectedTheme = 0; // default System
            int nightMode = AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_FOLLOW_SYSTEM;

            if (checkedId == R.id.radio_theme_light) {
                selectedTheme = 1;
                nightMode = AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_NO;
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.radio_theme_dark) {
                selectedTheme = 2;
                nightMode = AppCompatDelegate.MODE_NIGHT_YES;
            }

            // Save preference
            prefs.edit().putInt(PREF_THEME_KEY, selectedTheme).apply();

            // Apply immediately
            AppCompatDelegate.setDefaultNightMode(nightMode);
        });
    }

    private void setupNotificationSelection() {
        notifToggleListener = (buttonView, isChecked) -> {
            // Save preference
            prefs.edit().putBoolean(PREF_NOTIF_KEY, isChecked).apply();

            // The system permission can still be missing even though the user
            // wants reminders — route them to the system notification settings.
            if (isChecked && !NotificationManagerCompat.from(requireContext())
                    .areNotificationsEnabled()) {
                NotificationHelper.openNotificationSettings(requireContext());
            }
        };

        binding.switchNotifications.setOnCheckedChangeListener(notifToggleListener);
        refreshNotificationToggle();
    }

    /**
     * The switch reflects the effective reminder state: the user preference
     * AND the required system permission. Re-checked on resume so enabling
     * via system settings after this screen is left is picked up.
     */
    private void refreshNotificationToggle() {
        if (binding == null) return;

        boolean prefEnabled = prefs.getBoolean(PREF_NOTIF_KEY, true);
        boolean systemGranted = NotificationManagerCompat.from(requireContext())
                .areNotificationsEnabled();
        boolean effectiveEnabled = prefEnabled && systemGranted;

        if (binding.switchNotifications.isChecked() == effectiveEnabled) return;

        // Detach to avoid the listener re-firing while syncing the visual state
        binding.switchNotifications.setOnCheckedChangeListener(null);
        binding.switchNotifications.setChecked(effectiveEnabled);
        binding.switchNotifications.setOnCheckedChangeListener(notifToggleListener);
    }

    @Override
    public void onResume() {
        super.onResume();
        refreshNotificationToggle();
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/TaskAdapter.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Paint;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.ItemTaskBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.AnimUtil;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public class TaskAdapter extends RecyclerView.Adapter<TaskAdapter.TaskViewHolder> {

    public interface OnTaskActionListener {
        void onTaskCheckedChanged(Task task, boolean isChecked);
        void onTaskClick(Task task);
        void onTaskDelete(Task task);
        void onTaskShare(Task task);
        void onTaskArchive(Task task);
    }

    private List<Task> tasks = new ArrayList<>();
    private final OnTaskActionListener listener;
    private final SimpleDateFormat sdf =
            new SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy · hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault());

    public TaskAdapter(OnTaskActionListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public void setTasks(List<Task> tasks) {
        this.tasks = new ArrayList<>(tasks);
        notifyDataSetChanged();
    }

    public List<Task> getTasks() {
        return tasks;
    }

    public Task getTaskAt(int position) {
        if (position >= 0 && position < tasks.size()) {
            return tasks.get(position);
        }
        return null;
    }

    public void removeAt(int position) {
        if (position >= 0 && position < tasks.size()) {
            tasks.remove(position);
            notifyItemRemoved(position);
        }
    }

    @NonNull
    @Override
    public TaskViewHolder onCreateViewHolder(@NonNull ViewGroup parent, int viewType) {
        ItemTaskBinding binding = ItemTaskBinding.inflate(
                LayoutInflater.from(parent.getContext()), parent, false);
        return new TaskViewHolder(binding);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBindViewHolder(@NonNull TaskViewHolder holder, int position) {
        holder.bind(tasks.get(position));
        holder.fadeIn(position);
    }

    @Override
    public int getItemCount() {
        return tasks.size();
    }

    /** Maps a category to its pastel gradient card background drawable. */
    private int categoryCardBackground(String category) {
        if (category == null) return R.drawable.card_pastel_neutral;
        switch (category) {
            case "Work":
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_lilac;
            case "Personal":
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_mint;
            case "School":
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_peach;
            case "Wellness":
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_sky;
            case "Urgent":
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_rose;
            case "General":
            default:
                return R.drawable.card_pastel_neutral;
        }
    }

    class TaskViewHolder extends RecyclerView.ViewHolder {
        private final ItemTaskBinding binding;
        private boolean animatedIn;

        TaskViewHolder(ItemTaskBinding binding) {
            super(binding.getRoot());
            this.binding = binding;
        }

        /** Subtle fade-in, run once per ViewHolder instance. */
        void fadeIn(int position) {
            animatedIn = AnimUtil.fadeInOnce(binding.getRoot(), position, animatedIn);
        }

        void bind(Task task) {
            Context ctx = binding.getRoot().getContext();

            // Pastel gradient card background per category (24dp rounded)
            binding.taskCardBg.setBackgroundResource(categoryCardBackground(task.getCategory()));

            // Title
            binding.textTaskTitle.setText(task.getTitle());

            // Strikethrough if completed
            if (task.isCompleted()) {
                binding.textTaskTitle.setPaintFlags(
                        binding.textTaskTitle.getPaintFlags() | Paint.STRIKE_THRU_TEXT_FLAG);
                binding.textTaskTitle.setAlpha(0.6f);
            } else {
                binding.textTaskTitle.setPaintFlags(
                        binding.textTaskTitle.getPaintFlags() & (~Paint.STRIKE_THRU_TEXT_FLAG));
                binding.textTaskTitle.setAlpha(1.0f);
            }

            // Description
            if (task.getDescription() != null && !task.getDescription().isEmpty()) {
                binding.textTaskDescription.setText(task.getDescription());
                binding.textTaskDescription.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
            } else {
                binding.textTaskDescription.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            // Deadline
            if (task.getDeadline() > 0) {
                binding.deadlineContainer.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                binding.textTaskDeadline.setText(sdf.format(new Date(task.getDeadline())));
            } else {
                binding.deadlineContainer.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            // Category Badge
            if (task.getCategory() != null && !task.getCategory().isEmpty()) {
                binding.textTaskCategory.setText(task.getCategory());
                binding.textTaskCategory.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);

                int textColor;
                switch (task.getCategory()) {
                    case "Work":
                        textColor = R.color.lf_pastel_lilac_deep;
                        break;
                    case "Personal":
                        textColor = R.color.lf_pastel_mint_deep;
                        break;
                    case "School":
                        textColor = R.color.lf_pastel_peach_deep;
                        break;
                    case "Wellness":
                        textColor = R.color.lf_pastel_sky_deep;
                        break;
                    case "Urgent":
                        textColor = R.color.lf_pastel_rose_deep;
                        break;
                    case "General":
                    default:
                        textColor = R.color.lf_text_secondary;
                        break;
                }
                binding.textTaskCategory.setTextColor(ContextCompat.getColor(ctx, textColor));
                binding.textTaskCategory.getBackground()
                        .setTint(ContextCompat.getColor(ctx, R.color.lf_badge_tint));
            } else {
                binding.textTaskCategory.setVisibility(View.GONE);
            }

            // Checkbox
            binding.checkboxTask.setOnCheckedChangeListener(null); // clear old listener
            binding.checkboxTask.setChecked(task.isCompleted());
            binding.checkboxTask.setOnCheckedChangeListener((buttonView, isChecked) -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onTaskCheckedChanged(task, isChecked);
                }
            });

            // Click
            binding.getRoot().setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onTaskClick(task);
                }
            });

            // Share click
            binding.btnShareTask.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onTaskShare(task);
                }
            });

            // Archive click
            binding.btnArchiveTask.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onTaskArchive(task);
                }
            });

            // Delete click
            binding.btnDeleteTask.setOnClickListener(v -> {
                if (listener != null) {
                    listener.onTaskDelete(task);
                }
            });
        }
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/ui/TasksFragment.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.ui;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.DatePickerDialog;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.app.TimePickerDialog;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.text.Editable;
import android.text.TextWatcher;
import android.view.LayoutInflater;
import android.view.Menu;
import android.view.MenuInflater;
import android.view.MenuItem;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.widget.Toast;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
import androidx.core.view.MenuProvider;
import androidx.fragment.app.Fragment;
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.ItemTouchHelper;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.LinearLayoutManager;
import androidx.recyclerview.widget.RecyclerView;

import com.google.android.material.dialog.MaterialAlertDialogBuilder;
import com.google.android.material.snackbar.Snackbar;
import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.AppDatabase;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.TaskDao;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.DialogAddTaskBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.databinding.FragmentTasksBinding;
import com.lorenzflow.app.notification.AlarmReceiver;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.NotesFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.ui.PomodoroFragment;
import com.lorenzflow.app.util.PdfExportUtil;

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Calendar;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class TasksFragment extends Fragment implements TaskAdapter.OnTaskActionListener {

    private FragmentTasksBinding binding;
    private TaskAdapter activeAdapter;
    private TaskAdapter completedAdapter;
    private TaskDao taskDao;
    private boolean completedExpanded = false;
    private android.content.SharedPreferences prefs;
    private String selectedFilterCategory = "All";
    private String searchQuery = "";

    private final SimpleDateFormat sdf =
            new SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy · hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault());

    @Nullable
    @Override
    public View onCreateView(@NonNull LayoutInflater inflater,
                             @Nullable ViewGroup container,
                             @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        binding = FragmentTasksBinding.inflate(inflater, container, false);
        return binding.getRoot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onViewCreated(@NonNull View view, @Nullable Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        super.onViewCreated(view, savedInstanceState);

        taskDao = AppDatabase.getInstance(requireContext()).taskDao();
        prefs = requireContext().getSharedPreferences("lorenz_flow_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE);

        setupRecyclerViews();
        setupSwipeToDelete();
        setupCompletedSection();
        setupFab();
        setupMenu();
        setupCategoryFilter();
        setupQuickActions();
        setupHeaderAvatar();
        setupSearch();

        binding.btnExportPdf.setOnClickListener(v -> exportPdf());
        binding.btnClearCompleted.setOnClickListener(v -> clearCompletedTasks());

        loadTasks();
    }
