    /** Shows the saved profile photo (same source as ProfileFragment) or the
     *  default avatar when no photo was chosen yet. */
    private void setupHeaderAvatar() {
        String imageUri = prefs.getString("profile_image_uri", null);
        if (imageUri != null && !imageUri.isEmpty()) {
            binding.avatarImage.setImageURI(Uri.parse(imageUri));
            if (binding.avatarImage.getDrawable() == null) {
                binding.avatarImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
            }
        } else {
            binding.avatarImage.setImageResource(R.drawable.ic_default_avatar);
        }
    }

    /** Quick-action tiles on the redesigned header. */
    private void setupQuickActions() {
        binding.quickAddTask.setOnClickListener(v -> showTaskDialog(null));
        binding.quickNewNote.setOnClickListener(v ->
                requireActivity().getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                        .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new NotesFragment())
                        .addToBackStack(null)
                        .commit());
        binding.quickStartFocus.setOnClickListener(v ->
                requireActivity().getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                        .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new PomodoroFragment())
                        .addToBackStack(null)
                        .commit());
    }

    /** Client-side title search over the already-loaded task lists. */
    private void setupSearch() {
        binding.editSearch.addTextChangedListener(new TextWatcher() {
            @Override public void beforeTextChanged(CharSequence s, int a, int b, int c) { }
            @Override public void onTextChanged(CharSequence s, int a, int b, int c) { }
            @Override
            public void afterTextChanged(Editable s) {
                String query = s.toString().trim();
                if (query.equals(searchQuery)) return;
                searchQuery = query;
                loadTasks();
            }
        });
    }

    private void setupCategoryFilter() {
        binding.chipGroupFilter.setOnCheckedStateChangeListener((group, checkedIds) -> {
            if (checkedIds.isEmpty()) return;
            int checkedId = checkedIds.get(0);
            if (checkedId == R.id.filter_all) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "All";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_general) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "General";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_work) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "Work";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_personal) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "Personal";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_school) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "School";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_wellness) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "Wellness";
            } else if (checkedId == R.id.filter_urgent) {
                selectedFilterCategory = "Urgent";
            }
            loadTasks();
        });
    }

    private void setupRecyclerViews() {
        // Active tasks
        activeAdapter = new TaskAdapter(this);
        binding.recyclerActiveTasks.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(requireContext()));
        binding.recyclerActiveTasks.setAdapter(activeAdapter);

        // Completed tasks
        completedAdapter = new TaskAdapter(this);
        binding.recyclerCompletedTasks.setLayoutManager(new LinearLayoutManager(requireContext()));
        binding.recyclerCompletedTasks.setAdapter(completedAdapter);
    }

    private void setupSwipeToDelete() {
        ItemTouchHelper.SimpleCallback swipeCallback =
                new ItemTouchHelper.SimpleCallback(0, ItemTouchHelper.LEFT) {
            @Override
            public boolean onMove(@NonNull RecyclerView recyclerView,
                                  @NonNull RecyclerView.ViewHolder viewHolder,
                                  @NonNull RecyclerView.ViewHolder target) {
                return false;
            }

            @Override
            public void onSwiped(@NonNull RecyclerView.ViewHolder viewHolder, int direction) {
                int position = viewHolder.getAdapterPosition();
                Task task = activeAdapter.getTaskAt(position);
                if (task == null) return;

                activeAdapter.removeAt(position);
                updateEmptyState();

                // Cancel alarm
                cancelAlarm(task);

                Snackbar.make(binding.getRoot(), R.string.task_deleted, Snackbar.LENGTH_LONG)
                        .setAction(R.string.undo, v -> {
                            // Re-insert on undo
                            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                                taskDao.insert(task);
                                if (task.getDeadline() > 0 && task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis()) {
                                    scheduleAlarm(task);
                                }
                                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(TasksFragment.this::loadTasks);
                            });
                        })
                        .addCallback(new Snackbar.Callback() {
                            @Override
                            public void onDismissed(Snackbar snackbar, int event) {
                                if (event != DISMISS_EVENT_ACTION) {
                                    // Actually delete from DB if not undone
                                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> taskDao.delete(task));
                                }
                            }
                        })
                        .show();
            }
        };

        new ItemTouchHelper(swipeCallback).attachToRecyclerView(binding.recyclerActiveTasks);
    }

    private void setupCompletedSection() {
        binding.completedHeader.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            completedExpanded = !completedExpanded;
            binding.recyclerCompletedTasks.setVisibility(
                    completedExpanded ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
            binding.expandIcon.setImageResource(
                    completedExpanded ? R.drawable.ic_expand_less : R.drawable.ic_expand_more);
        });
    }

    private void setupFab() {
        binding.fabAddTask.setOnClickListener(v -> showTaskDialog(null));
    }

    private void setupMenu() {
        requireActivity().addMenuProvider(new MenuProvider() {
            @Override
            public void onCreateMenu(@NonNull Menu menu, @NonNull MenuInflater menuInflater) {
                menuInflater.inflate(R.menu.menu_tasks, menu);
            }

            @Override
            public boolean onMenuItemSelected(@NonNull MenuItem menuItem) {
                int id = menuItem.getItemId();
                if (id == R.id.action_export_pdf) {
                    exportPdf();
                    return true;
                } else if (id == R.id.action_view_archive) {
                    requireActivity().getSupportFragmentManager().beginTransaction()
                            .replace(R.id.fragment_container, new ArchiveTasksFragment())
                            .addToBackStack(null)
                            .commit();
                    return true;
                } else if (id == R.id.sort_deadline) {
                    prefs.edit().putInt("pref_tasks_sort", 0).apply();
                    loadTasks();
                    return true;
                } else if (id == R.id.sort_newest) {
                    prefs.edit().putInt("pref_tasks_sort", 1).apply();
                    loadTasks();
                    return true;
                } else if (id == R.id.sort_oldest) {
                    prefs.edit().putInt("pref_tasks_sort", 2).apply();
                    loadTasks();
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
        }, getViewLifecycleOwner(), Lifecycle.State.RESUMED);
    }

    private void loadTasks() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Task> active = taskDao.getActiveTasks();
            List<Task> completed = taskDao.getCompletedTasks();

            // Hero counts every active task due today, before filters/sorting
            final int dueToday = countDueToday(active);

            // Filter by category if not "All"
            if (!selectedFilterCategory.equals("All")) {
                active.removeIf(t -> !selectedFilterCategory.equalsIgnoreCase(t.getCategory()));
                completed.removeIf(t -> !selectedFilterCategory.equalsIgnoreCase(t.getCategory()));
            }

            // Filter by search query (title match)
            if (!searchQuery.isEmpty()) {
                String q = searchQuery.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
                active.removeIf(t -> t.getTitle() == null
                        || !t.getTitle().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q));
                completed.removeIf(t -> t.getTitle() == null
                        || !t.getTitle().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q));
            }

            // Apply Sort
            sortTasksList(active);

            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                activeAdapter.setTasks(active);
                completedAdapter.setTasks(completed);
                updateHeroDueToday(dueToday);

                // Completed section visibility
                if (completed.isEmpty()) {
                    binding.completedSection.setVisibility(View.GONE);
                } else {
                    binding.completedSection.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
                    binding.completedLabel.setText(
                            getString(R.string.completed_tasks, completed.size()));
                }

                updateEmptyState();
            });
        });
    }

    /** 
     * Number of active tasks whose deadline falls on today's calendar date.
     */
    private int countDueToday(List<Task> tasks) {
        Calendar start = Calendar.getInstance();
        start.set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 0);
        start.set(Calendar.MINUTE, 0);
        start.set(Calendar.SECOND, 0);
        start.set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0);
        Calendar end = (Calendar) start.clone();
        end.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, 1);
        long startMs = start.getTimeInMillis();
        long endMs = end.getTimeInMillis();

        int count = 0;
        for (Task t : tasks) {
            long d = t.getDeadline();
            if (d >= startMs && d < endMs) {
                count++;
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    private void updateHeroDueToday(int count) {
        if (binding == null || getActivity() == null) return;
        binding.textHeroCount.setText(String.valueOf(count));
        binding.textHeroDueLabel.setText(
                getResources().getQuantityString(R.plurals.hero_tasks_due, count, count));
    }

    private void sortTasksList(List<Task> tasks) {
        int sortType = prefs.getInt("pref_tasks_sort", 0); // 0 = Default (deadline), 1 = Newest, 2 = Oldest
        if (sortType == 1) {
            tasks.sort((t1, t2) -> Long.compare(t2.getCreatedAt(), t1.getCreatedAt()));
        } else if (sortType == 2) {
            tasks.sort((t1, t2) -> Long.compare(t1.getCreatedAt(), t2.getCreatedAt()));
        } else {
            // Default: tasks with deadlines first (ascending), then by newest created
            tasks.sort((t1, t2) -> {
                long d1 = t1.getDeadline();
                long d2 = t2.getDeadline();
                if (d1 > 0 && d2 > 0) {
                    return Long.compare(d1, d2);
                } else if (d1 > 0) {
                    return -1;
                } else if (d2 > 0) {
                    return 1;
                } else {
                    return Long.compare(t2.getCreatedAt(), t1.getCreatedAt());
                }
            });
        }
    }

    private void updateEmptyState() {
        boolean empty = activeAdapter.getItemCount() == 0
                && completedAdapter.getItemCount() == 0;
        binding.emptyState.setVisibility(empty ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
        updateScrollability();
    }

    private void updateScrollability() {
        if (binding == null) return;
        binding.nestedScrollView.getViewTreeObserver().addOnGlobalLayoutListener(
                new android.view.ViewTreeObserver.OnGlobalLayoutListener() {
                    @Override
                    public void onGlobalLayout() {
                        if (binding == null) return;
                        binding.nestedScrollView.getViewTreeObserver().removeOnGlobalLayoutListener(this);
                        View child = binding.nestedScrollView.getChildAt(0);
                        if (child != null) {
                            boolean canScroll = child.getHeight() > binding.nestedScrollView.getHeight();
                            if (binding.nestedScrollView.isNestedScrollingEnabled() != canScroll) {
                                binding.nestedScrollView.setNestedScrollingEnabled(canScroll);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
    }

    // ==================== ADD TASK DIALOG ====================

    private void showTaskDialog(@Nullable Task existingTask) {
        DialogAddTaskBinding dialogBinding = DialogAddTaskBinding.inflate(getLayoutInflater());

        final long[] selectedDeadline = {existingTask != null ? existingTask.getDeadline() : 0};

        if (existingTask != null) {
            dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.setText(existingTask.getTitle());
            dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.setText(existingTask.getDescription());
            if (existingTask.getDeadline() > 0) {
                dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setText(sdf.format(new Date(existingTask.getDeadline())));
            }

            // Pre-select category
            String category = existingTask.getCategory();
            if (category != null) {
                switch (category) {
                    case "Work":
                        dialogBinding.chipWork.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                    case "Personal":
                        dialogBinding.chipPersonal.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                    case "School":
                        dialogBinding.chipSchool.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                    case "Wellness":
                        dialogBinding.chipWellness.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                    case "Urgent":
                        dialogBinding.chipUrgent.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                    case "General":
                    default:
                        dialogBinding.chipGeneral.setChecked(true);
                        break;
                }
            }
        }

        // Deadline picker button
        dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
            if (selectedDeadline[0] > 0) {
                cal.setTimeInMillis(selectedDeadline[0]);
            }
            new DatePickerDialog(requireContext(), (view, year, month, day) -> {
                Calendar deadlineCal = Calendar.getInstance();
                deadlineCal.set(year, month, day);

                // Also pick time
                new TimePickerDialog(requireContext(), (timeView, hour, minute) -> {
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, hour);
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.MINUTE, minute);
                    deadlineCal.set(Calendar.SECOND, 0);
                    selectedDeadline[0] = deadlineCal.getTimeInMillis();
                    dialogBinding.btnSetDeadline.setText(sdf.format(new Date(selectedDeadline[0])));
                }, cal.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY), cal.get(Calendar.MINUTE), false).show();
            }, cal.get(Calendar.YEAR), cal.get(Calendar.MONTH), cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)).show();
        });

        String titleStr = existingTask != null ? getString(R.string.edit_task) : getString(R.string.add_task);

        MaterialAlertDialogBuilder builder = new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(titleStr)
                .setView(dialogBinding.getRoot())
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.save, (dialog, which) -> {
                    String title = dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editTaskTitle.getText().toString().trim() : "";
                    String desc = dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.getText() != null
                            ? dialogBinding.editTaskDescription.getText().toString().trim() : "";

                    if (title.isEmpty()) return;

                    // Get selected category
                    String category = "General";
                    int checkedChipId = dialogBinding.chipGroupCategory.getCheckedChipId();
                    if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_work) {
                        category = "Work";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_personal) {
                        category = "Personal";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_school) {
                        category = "School";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_wellness) {
                        category = "Wellness";
                    } else if (checkedChipId == R.id.chip_urgent) {
                        category = "Urgent";
                    }

                    Task task = existingTask != null ? existingTask : new Task();
                    task.setTitle(title);
                    task.setDescription(desc);
                    task.setDeadline(selectedDeadline[0]);
                    task.setCategory(category);

                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        if (existingTask != null) {
                            taskDao.update(task);
                            cancelAlarm(task);
                            if (task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis() && !task.isCompleted()) {
                                scheduleAlarm(task);
                            }
                        } else {
                            long id = taskDao.insert(task);
                            task.setId(id);
                            if (task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis()) {
                                scheduleAlarm(task);
                            }
                        }

                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadTasks);
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null);

        if (existingTask != null) {
            builder.setNeutralButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) -> {
                deleteTaskConfirm(existingTask);
            });
        }

        builder.show();
    }

    private void deleteTaskConfirm(Task task) {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(R.string.delete)
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.delete_task_confirm, task.getTitle()))
                .setPositiveButton(R.string.delete, (dialog, which) -> {
                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        taskDao.delete(task);
                        cancelAlarm(task);
                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                                Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.task_deleted, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                                loadTasks();
                            });
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    // ==================== ALARM MANAGEMENT ====================

    private void scheduleAlarm(Task task) {
        Context ctx = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (alarmManager == null) return;

        Intent intent = new Intent(ctx, AlarmReceiver.class);
        intent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_ID, (int) task.getId());
        intent.putExtra(AlarmReceiver.EXTRA_TASK_TITLE, task.getTitle());

        PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                ctx, (int) task.getId(), intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);

        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                if (alarmManager.canScheduleExactAlarms()) {
                    alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                            AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
                } else {
                    alarmManager.setAndAllowWhileIdle(
                            AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
                }
            } else {
                alarmManager.setExactAndAllowWhileIdle(
                        AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
            }
        } catch (SecurityException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            // Fallback to inexact alarm if permission is missing/revoked
            alarmManager.setAndAllowWhileIdle(
                    AlarmManager.RTC_WAKEUP, task.getDeadline(), pi);
        }
    }

    private void cancelAlarm(Task task) {
        Context ctx = requireContext().getApplicationContext();
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) ctx.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        if (alarmManager == null) return;

        Intent intent = new Intent(ctx, AlarmReceiver.class);
        PendingIntent pi = PendingIntent.getBroadcast(
                ctx, (int) task.getId(), intent,
                PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT | PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE);
        alarmManager.cancel(pi);
    }

    // ==================== TASK CALLBACKS ====================

    @Override
    public void onTaskCheckedChanged(Task task, boolean isChecked) {
        task.setCompleted(isChecked);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            taskDao.update(task);
            if (isChecked) {
                cancelAlarm(task);
            } else if (task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis()) {
                scheduleAlarm(task);
            }
            if (getActivity() != null) {
                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadTasks);
            }
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskClick(Task task) {
        showTaskDialog(task);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskDelete(Task task) {
        deleteTaskConfirm(task);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskShare(Task task) {
        Intent sendIntent = new Intent();
        sendIntent.setAction(Intent.ACTION_SEND);
        
        String shareContent = task.getTitle();
        if (task.getDescription() != null && !task.getDescription().isEmpty()) {
            shareContent += "\n\n" + task.getDescription();
        }
        if (task.getDeadline() > 0) {
            shareContent += "\n\nDeadline: " + sdf.format(new Date(task.getDeadline()));
        }
        
        sendIntent.putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, shareContent);
        sendIntent.setType("text/plain");
        Intent shareIntent = Intent.createChooser(sendIntent, getString(R.string.transfer_task_to));
        startActivity(shareIntent);
    }

    @Override
    public void onTaskArchive(Task task) {
        task.setArchived(true);
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            taskDao.update(task);
            cancelAlarm(task);
            if (getActivity() == null) return;
            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                loadTasks();
                Snackbar.make(binding.getRoot(), R.string.task_archived, Snackbar.LENGTH_LONG)
                        .setAction(R.string.undo, v -> {
                            task.setArchived(false);
                            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                                taskDao.update(task);
                                if (task.getDeadline() > 0 && task.getDeadline() > System.currentTimeMillis() && !task.isCompleted()) {
                                    scheduleAlarm(task);
                                }
                                if (getActivity() != null) {
                                    requireActivity().runOnUiThread(TasksFragment.this::loadTasks);
                                }
                            });
                        })
                        .show();
            });
        });
    }

    private void clearCompletedTasks() {
        new MaterialAlertDialogBuilder(requireContext())
                .setTitle(getString(R.string.clear_completed_title))
                .setMessage(getString(R.string.clear_completed_message))
                .setPositiveButton(getString(R.string.clear_all), (dialog, which) -> {
                    Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
                        taskDao.deleteAllCompleted();
                        if (getActivity() != null) {
                            requireActivity().runOnUiThread(this::loadTasks);
                        }
                    });
                })
                .setNegativeButton(R.string.cancel, null)
                .show();
    }

    // ==================== PDF EXPORT ====================

    private void exportPdf() {
        Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor().execute(() -> {
            List<Task> tasks = taskDao.getActiveTasks();

            if (tasks.isEmpty()) {
                if (getActivity() != null) {
                    requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() ->
                            Toast.makeText(requireContext(), R.string.pdf_no_tasks, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show());
                }
                return;
            }

            boolean success = PdfExportUtil.exportTasks(requireContext(), tasks);

            if (getActivity() != null) {
                requireActivity().runOnUiThread(() -> {
                    Toast.makeText(requireContext(),
                            success ? R.string.pdf_exported : R.string.pdf_export_failed,
                            Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show();
                });
            }
        });
    }

    @Override
    public void onDestroyView() {
        super.onDestroyView();
        binding = null;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/util/AnimUtil.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.util;

import android.view.View;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

/**
 * Small shared view animations for the soft-and-airy polish pass.
 */
public final class AnimUtil {

    private static final long FADE_DURATION_MS = 200L;
    private static final long STAGGER_MS = 28L;

    private AnimUtil() {
    }

    /**
     * Subtle fade-in for list items: plays at most once per ViewHolder so
     * recycled rows and data reloads don't flicker. Pass the ViewHolder's
     * current flag in and store the returned value back on it.
     */
    public static boolean fadeInOnce(@NonNull View view, int position, boolean alreadyAnimated) {
        if (alreadyAnimated) {
            // Rebinds of an already-seen row stay fully opaque
            view.setAlpha(1f);
        } else {
            view.setAlpha(0f);
            view.animate()
                    .alpha(1f)
                    .setDuration(FADE_DURATION_MS)
                    // Stagger the first screenful slightly; scrolled-in rows start at 0
                    .setStartDelay(Math.min(position, 6) * STAGGER_MS)
                    .start();
        }
        return true;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/util/PdfExportUtil.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.util;

import android.content.ContentValues;
import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Canvas;
import android.graphics.Paint;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Environment;
import android.provider.MediaStore;

import com.lorenzflow.app.R;
import com.lorenzflow.app.data.Task;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Generates a cleanly formatted PDF of active tasks and saves it to the Documents directory.
 */
public class PdfExportUtil {

    private static final int PAGE_WIDTH = 595;   // A4 width in points
    private static final int PAGE_HEIGHT = 842;  // A4 height in points
    private static final int MARGIN = 50;
    private static final int CONTENT_WIDTH = PAGE_WIDTH - 2 * MARGIN;

    /**
     * Exports the given task list as a PDF file.
     *
     * @param context Application context
     * @param tasks   List of active tasks to export
     * @return true if the PDF was saved successfully
     */
    public static boolean exportTasks(Context context, List<Task> tasks) {
        PdfDocument document = new PdfDocument();
        SimpleDateFormat sdf = new SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy 'at' hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault());
        SimpleDateFormat fileSdf = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss", Locale.getDefault());

        // Paints
        Paint titlePaint = new Paint();
        titlePaint.setTypeface(Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD));
        titlePaint.setTextSize(22);
        titlePaint.setColor(0xFF1B1B1F);
        titlePaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint subtitlePaint = new Paint();
        subtitlePaint.setTextSize(10);
        subtitlePaint.setColor(0xFF777680);
        subtitlePaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint headerPaint = new Paint();
        headerPaint.setTypeface(Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD));
        headerPaint.setTextSize(12);
        headerPaint.setColor(0xFF46464F);
        headerPaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint taskTitlePaint = new Paint();
        taskTitlePaint.setTypeface(Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD));
        taskTitlePaint.setTextSize(13);
        taskTitlePaint.setColor(0xFF1B1B1F);
        taskTitlePaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint bodyPaint = new Paint();
        bodyPaint.setTextSize(11);
        bodyPaint.setColor(0xFF46464F);
        bodyPaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint deadlinePaint = new Paint();
        deadlinePaint.setTextSize(10);
        deadlinePaint.setColor(0xFF5B5FE3);
        deadlinePaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        Paint linePaint = new Paint();
        linePaint.setColor(0xFFC7C5D0);
        linePaint.setStrokeWidth(0.5f);

        Paint bulletPaint = new Paint();
        bulletPaint.setColor(0xFF5B5FE3);
        bulletPaint.setAntiAlias(true);

        // Create page
        int pageNum = 1;
        PdfDocument.PageInfo pageInfo = new PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, pageNum).create();
        PdfDocument.Page page = document.startPage(pageInfo);
        Canvas canvas = page.getCanvas();

        float y = MARGIN;

        // Title
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_title), MARGIN, y + 22, titlePaint);
        y += 32;

        // Date
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_generated_on, sdf.format(new Date())), MARGIN, y + 10, subtitlePaint);
        y += 20;

        // Divider
        canvas.drawLine(MARGIN, y, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN, y, linePaint);
        y += 20;

        // Column headers
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_col_task), MARGIN + 15, y, headerPaint);
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_col_deadline), PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN - 120, y, headerPaint);
        y += 8;
        canvas.drawLine(MARGIN, y, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN, y, linePaint);
        y += 16;

        // Task rows
        for (int i = 0; i < tasks.size(); i++) {
            Task task = tasks.get(i);

            // Check if we need a new page
            if (y > PAGE_HEIGHT - MARGIN - 60) {
                document.finishPage(page);
                pageNum++;
                pageInfo = new PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, pageNum).create();
                page = document.startPage(pageInfo);
                canvas = page.getCanvas();
                y = MARGIN;
            }

            // Bullet
            canvas.drawCircle(MARGIN + 5, y - 4, 3, bulletPaint);

            // Task title
            String title = task.getTitle();
            if (title.length() > 40) title = title.substring(0, 37) + "...";
            canvas.drawText(title, MARGIN + 15, y, taskTitlePaint);

            // Deadline
            if (task.getDeadline() > 0) {
                String deadline = sdf.format(new Date(task.getDeadline()));
                canvas.drawText(deadline, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN - 120, y, deadlinePaint);
            } else {
                canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_no_deadline), PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN - 120, y, bodyPaint);
            }

            y += 6;

            // Description (if present)
            if (task.getDescription() != null && !task.getDescription().isEmpty()) {
                String desc = task.getDescription();
                if (desc.length() > 80) desc = desc.substring(0, 77) + "...";
                canvas.drawText(desc, MARGIN + 15, y + 11, bodyPaint);
                y += 16;
            }

            y += 14;

            // Light separator
            canvas.drawLine(MARGIN + 15, y - 4, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN, y - 4, linePaint);
            y += 6;
        }

        // Footer
        if (y > PAGE_HEIGHT - MARGIN - 30) {
            document.finishPage(page);
            pageNum++;
            pageInfo = new PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, pageNum).create();
            page = document.startPage(pageInfo);
            canvas = page.getCanvas();
            y = MARGIN;
        }
        y = PAGE_HEIGHT - MARGIN;
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_total_tasks, tasks.size()), MARGIN, y, subtitlePaint);
        canvas.drawText(context.getString(R.string.pdf_footer_version), PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN - 85, y, subtitlePaint);

        document.finishPage(page);

        // Save the file
        String fileName = "LorenzFlow_Tasks_" + fileSdf.format(new Date()) + ".pdf";
        boolean success = false;

        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                // Use MediaStore for API 29+
                ContentValues values = new ContentValues();
                values.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, fileName);
                values.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, "application/pdf");
                values.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS);

                Uri uri = context.getContentResolver().insert(
                        MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external"), values);

                if (uri != null) {
                    try (OutputStream os = context.getContentResolver().openOutputStream(uri)) {
                        document.writeTo(os);
                        success = true;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Legacy file access for API 26-28
                File dir = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOCUMENTS);
                if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs();

                File file = new File(dir, fileName);
                try (FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(file)) {
                    document.writeTo(fos);
                    success = true;
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        } finally {
            document.close();
        }

        return success;
    }
}


########## ./app/src/main/java/com/lorenzflow/app/util/SecurityUtil.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.util;

import android.security.keystore.KeyGenParameterSpec;
import android.security.keystore.KeyProperties;
import android.util.Base64;

import java.security.KeyStore;

import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator;
import javax.crypto.SecretKey;
import javax.crypto.spec.IvParameterSpec;

public class SecurityUtil {

    private static final String ANDROID_KEY_STORE = "AndroidKeyStore";
    private static final String KEY_ALIAS = "LorenzFlowSecureKey";
    private static final String TRANSFORMATION = "AES/CBC/PKCS7Padding";

    private static synchronized void generateKey() throws Exception {
        KeyStore keyStore = KeyStore.getInstance(ANDROID_KEY_STORE);
        keyStore.load(null);
        if (!keyStore.containsAlias(KEY_ALIAS)) {
            KeyGenerator keyGenerator = KeyGenerator.getInstance(
                    KeyProperties.KEY_ALGORITHM_AES, ANDROID_KEY_STORE);
            keyGenerator.init(new KeyGenParameterSpec.Builder(
                    KEY_ALIAS,
                    KeyProperties.PURPOSE_ENCRYPT | KeyProperties.PURPOSE_DECRYPT)
                    .setBlockModes(KeyProperties.BLOCK_MODE_CBC)
                    .setEncryptionPaddings(KeyProperties.ENCRYPTION_PADDING_PKCS7)
                    .setRandomizedEncryptionRequired(true)
                    .build());
            keyGenerator.generateKey();
        }
    }

    private static SecretKey getSecretKey() throws Exception {
        generateKey();
        KeyStore keyStore = KeyStore.getInstance(ANDROID_KEY_STORE);
        keyStore.load(null);
        return (SecretKey) keyStore.getKey(KEY_ALIAS, null);
    }

    public static class EncryptedData {
        public final String encryptedText;
        public final String iv;

        public EncryptedData(String encryptedText, String iv) {
            this.encryptedText = encryptedText;
            this.iv = iv;
        }
    }

    public static EncryptedData encrypt(String plainText) {
        try {
            Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(TRANSFORMATION);
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, getSecretKey());
            byte[] encryptedBytes = cipher.doFinal(plainText.getBytes("UTF-8"));
            String encryptedText = Base64.encodeToString(encryptedBytes, Base64.DEFAULT);
            String iv = Base64.encodeToString(cipher.getIV(), Base64.DEFAULT);
            return new EncryptedData(encryptedText, iv);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static String decrypt(String encryptedText, String ivStr) {
        try {
            byte[] encryptedBytes = Base64.decode(encryptedText, Base64.DEFAULT);
            byte[] iv = Base64.decode(ivStr, Base64.DEFAULT);
            Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(TRANSFORMATION);
            cipher.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, getSecretKey(), new IvParameterSpec(iv));
            byte[] decryptedBytes = cipher.doFinal(encryptedBytes);
            return new String(decryptedBytes, "UTF-8");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return null;
        }
    }
}

########## ./app/src/test/java/com/lorenzflow/app/data/EntitiesTest.java ##########
package com.lorenzflow.app.data;

import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertTrue;

import org.junit.Test;

/**
 * JVM unit tests for the Room entities' default values and getter/setter
 * round-trips — the pure-Java parts that run without a device.
 *
 * Note: SecurityUtil cannot be unit-tested on the JVM because it needs the
 * AndroidKeyStore provider and android.util.Base64 (both absent in plain
 * JUnit runs) — its behavior is covered by the instrumented SecurityUtilTest
 * under androidTest instead.
 */
public class EntitiesTest {

    @Test
    public void taskConstructor_setsDefaults() {
        Task task = new Task();
        assertEquals("General", task.getCategory());
        assertEquals(0, task.getDeadline());
        assertFalse(task.isCompleted());
        assertFalse(task.isArchived());
        assertTrue(task.getCreatedAt() > 0);
    }

    @Test
    public void taskSetters_roundtrip() {
        Task task = new Task();
        task.setTitle("title");
        task.setDescription("desc");
        task.setDeadline(12345L);
        task.setCompleted(true);
        task.setCategory("Work");
        task.setArchived(true);

        assertEquals("title", task.getTitle());
        assertEquals("desc", task.getDescription());
        assertEquals(12345L, task.getDeadline());
        assertTrue(task.isCompleted());
        assertEquals("Work", task.getCategory());
        assertTrue(task.isArchived());
    }

    @Test
    public void noteConstructor_setsDefaults() {
        Note note = new Note();
        assertEquals(0, note.getColor());
        assertEquals(0, note.getPosition());
        assertFalse(note.isArchived());
        assertTrue(note.getTimestamp() > 0);
    }

    @Test
    public void noteSetters_roundtrip() {
        Note note = new Note();
        note.setTitle("t");
        note.setBody("b");
        note.setColor(3);
        note.setPosition(7);
        note.setArchived(true);

        assertEquals("t", note.getTitle());
        assertEquals("b", note.getBody());
        assertEquals(3, note.getColor());
        assertEquals(7, note.getPosition());
        assertTrue(note.isArchived());
    }

    @Test
    public void passwordEntryConstructor_setsDefaults() {
        PasswordEntry entry = new PasswordEntry();
        assertTrue(entry.getCreatedAt() > 0);
        assertEquals(0, entry.getId()); // autogenerate, unset until insert
    }
}
